Question:
[math]-1+2 i[/math] সংখ্যাটির আরগী চিত্র কোনটি?

Options:
ক. <br/><img src="https://app.nexes.ai/storage/question-bank-images/2025/08/images2fhsc20math202nd2020paper20panjeeri20guide20part220chapter2003-2_1755591352_yf8pvG1S.png" alt="undefined"><br/>
খ. <br/><img src="https://app.nexes.ai/storage/question-bank-images/2025/08/images2fhsc20math202nd2020paper20panjeeri20guide20part220chapter2003-3_1755591354_E0mDjEgj.png" alt="undefined"><br/>
গ. <br/><img src="https://app.nexes.ai/storage/question-bank-images/2025/08/images2fhsc20math202nd2020paper20panjeeri20guide20part220chapter2003-4_1755591355_XtIBKEMH.png" alt="undefined"><br/>
ঘ. <br/><img src="https://app.nexes.ai/storage/question-bank-images/2025/08/images2fhsc20math202nd2020paper20panjeeri20guide20part220chapter2003-5_1755591356_FNrHOkzm.png" alt="undefined"><br/>


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
আমরা জানি, [math]a+i b[/math]
 জটিল সংখ্যার ক্রমজোড় [math](a,b)[/math]
 [math]\therefore-1+2 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 জটিল সংখ্যার ক্রমজোড় [math](-1, 2)[/math]
সুতরাং [math]- 1 + 2i[/math]
 সংখ্যাটির আরগাঁ চিত্র নিম্নরূপ:
[Image]

Question #2 (ID: 439645)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]i^{4n+1}[/math]এর মান কত? যেখানে [math]n[/math] যেকোনো পূর্ণসংখ্যা।

Options:
ক. [math]\text {-i}[/math]
খ. [math]\text{i}[/math]
গ. [math]-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]i^{4 n+1}=i^{4 n} \cdot i=\left(i^{4}\right)^{n} \cdot i=1^{n} \cdot i=1 \cdot i=i[/math]
 [math][\because i=\sqrt{-1}[/math]
 সুতরাং [math]\left.(i)^{4}=(\sqrt{-1})^{4}=1\right][/math]


Explanation:

Question #3 (ID: 439646)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]1+i[/math] এর মডুলাস কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-\sqrt{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
আমরা জানি, মডুলাস, [math]|x+i y \mid=\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 মডুলাস, [math]|1+i|=\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #4 (ID: 439647)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]1+i[/math] ও [math]1 - i[/math] এর গুণফল কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-2[/math]
খ. [math]-1[/math]
গ. [math]1[/math]
ঘ. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](1+i)(1-i)=1^{2}-i^{2}=1-(-1)=1+1=2[/math]

Question #5 (ID: 439648)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math](2+i)[/math] কে [math](2-i)[/math] দ্বারা ভাগ করলে ভাগফল কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{4+3 i}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{3+4 i}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3-4 i}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{-3+4 i}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{2+i}{2-i}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(2+i)(2+i)}{(2-i)(2+i)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(2+i)^{2}}{(2)^{2}-(i)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(2)^{2}+2.2 . i+(i)^{2}}{4-(-1)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{4+4 i-1}{4+1}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because i^{2}=-1\right][/math]
 [math]=\frac{3+4 i}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #7 (ID: 439650)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\overline{1+i}=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]1-\mathrm{i}[/math]
B. [math]\mathrm{i}-1[/math]
C. [math]1+\mathrm{i}[/math]
D. [math]1+\mathrm{i}^{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
আমরা জানি, অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যা জোড়ায় থাকে। যদি [math]a + ib[/math]
 একটি জটিল সংখ্যা হয় তবে এর অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যা [math]a – ib[/math]
 হবে ।[math]\therefore \overline{1+i}=1-\mathrm{i}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #8 (ID: 439651)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=2+i[/math] হলে, [math]\mathbf{z}+\overline{\mathbf{z}}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]4[/math]
B. [math]4+2 i[/math]
C. [math]4-2 i[/math]
D. [math]8[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]z=2+i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \bar{z}=2-\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [এখানে, [math]\bar{z}[/math]
 অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যা][math]\therefore \mathrm{z}+\overline{\mathrm{z}}=2+\mathrm{i}+2-\mathrm{i}=4[/math]


Explanation:

Question #9 (ID: 439615)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]x = 2 + i[/math] এবং [math]y = - 3 - 2i[/math] দুটি জটিল সংখ্যা ।

[math]x + iy[/math] কোন চতুৰ্ভাগে অবস্থিত?

Options:
ক. প্ৰথম
খ. দ্বিতীয়
গ. তৃতীয়
ঘ. চতুর্থ


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

[Image]
[math]x+i y[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(2+i)+i(-3-2 i) \\=2+i-3 i-2 i^{2}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]2-2i+2[/math]
 [math]4-2 i[/math]
Diagram এ [math]4-2 i[/math]
 ৪ র্থ এ অবস্থিত

Explanation:

Question #12 (ID: 439618)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]1, \omega, \omega^{2}[/math] এককের তিনটি ঘনমূল ।

[math]\frac{1}{\omega^{3}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{5}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{7}}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]-\omega^{2}[/math]
খ. [math]-\omega[/math]
গ. [math]\omega[/math]
ঘ. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{\omega^{3}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{5}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{7}}=\frac{\omega^{4}+\omega^{2}+1}{\omega^{7}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\omega^{3} \cdot \omega+\omega^{2}+1}{\omega^{7}}=\frac{1+\omega+\omega^{2}}{\omega^{7}}=\frac{0}{\omega^{7}}=0[/math]


Explanation:

Question #13 (ID: 439619)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]Z_{1}=-3 i[/math] এবং [math]Z_{2}=1+i[/math] [math]\mathbf{Z}_{1}, \mathbf{Z}_{2}[/math]এর মুখ্য আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-45^{\circ}[/math]
খ. [math]45^{\circ}[/math]
গ. [math]135^{\circ}[/math]
ঘ. [math]225^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]z_{1} \cdot z_{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(-3 i)(1+i) \\=-3 i-3 i^{2} \\=3-3 i\end{array}[/math]
এটা ৪র্থ চতুর্ভাগে অবস্থিত।তাই Argument [math]=-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{3}{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]=-\pi / 4=-45^{\circ} .[/math]


Explanation:

Question #14 (ID: 439620)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]Z_{1}=-3 i[/math] এবং [math]Z_{2}=1+i[/math] [math]\frac{z_{2}}{Z_{1}}[/math] এর পরমমান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{2}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\frac{z_{2}}{z_{1}}=\frac{1+i}{-3 i}[/math]
 [math]=-\frac{1}{3}+\frac{1}{3} i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { Modulus = }[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\left(-\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{3}\right)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #15 (ID: 439621)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\mathrm{Z}=2-3 \mathrm{i}[/math] একটি জটিল সংখ্যা এবং [math]\overline{\mathrm{Z}}[/math] এর অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যা ।

[math]|\mathrm{Z}+\overline{\mathrm{Z}}|[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]-4[/math]
খ. [math]4[/math]
গ. [math]i[/math]
ঘ. [math]6i[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]z=2+3 i[/math]
 হলে [math]\bar{z}=2-3 i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad z+\bar{z}=4[/math]
 [math]\therefore|z+\bar{z}|=4[/math]


Explanation:

Question #16 (ID: 439622)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]\mathrm{Z}=2-3 \mathrm{i}[/math] একটি জটিল সংখ্যা এবং [math]\overline{\mathrm{Z}}[/math] এর অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যা ।

[math](\mathrm{Z}-\overline{\mathbf{Z}})[/math] এর মুখ্য আর্গুমেন্ট কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\pi[/math]
খ. [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}z=2-3 i \\\bar{z}=2+3 i\end{array}[/math]
 [math]z-\bar{z}=2-3 i-2-3 i[/math]
 [math]=-6 i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { Argument }=-\frac{\pi}{2} \text {. }[/math]


Explanation:

Question #17 (ID: 439623)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]x^{2}-p x+q=0[/math] এবং [math]\sqrt{3 x+i}-x^{2}=0[/math] একই সমীকরণ নির্দেশ করে ।

[math](q-p)[/math] এর অবস্থান কোন চতুৰ্ভাগে?

Options:
ক. প্ৰথম
খ. দ্বিতীয়
গ. তৃতীয়
ঘ. চতুর্থ


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x^{2}-p x+q=0[/math]
এবং [math]\sqrt{3} x+i-x^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-\sqrt{3} x-i=0[/math]
 একই সমীকরণ হলে,[math]\mathrm{p}=\sqrt{3} ; \mathrm{q}=-\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\therefore q-p=-1-\sqrt{3}[/math]
 যা [math](-\sqrt{3},-1)[/math]
 বিন্দুকে নির্দেশ করে।

Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #18 (ID: 439624)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও :

[math]x^{2}-p x+q=0[/math] এবং [math]\sqrt{3 x+i}-x^{2}=0[/math] একই সমীকরণ নির্দেশ করে ।

[math]\left(p^{3}-q^{3}\right)[/math] এর মডুলাস কত?

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math]3 \sqrt{3}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \sqrt{7}[/math]
ঘ. [math]7 \sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x^{2}-p x+q=0[/math]
 এবং [math]\sqrt{3} x+i-x^{2}=0[/math]
 একই সমীকরণ[math]\Rightarrow-\sqrt{3} x-i+x^{2}=0[/math]
নির্দেশ করলে [math]\text { 1. } x[/math]
 ও [math]x^{2}[/math]
 সহগ সমান হবে,[math]\therefore \quad p=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]q=-i[/math]
 [math]\therefore p=\sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore p^{3}-q^{3}[/math]
 [math]=(\sqrt{3})^{3}-(-i)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=3 \sqrt{3}+i[/math]
 [math]\text { Modules }[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(3 \sqrt{3})^{2}+1^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=2 \sqrt{7} \text {. }[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #19 (ID: 439625)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]p=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math] ও [math]4=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})[/math] [math](1+p)(1+q)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-1[/math]
খ. [math]1[/math]
গ. [math]\omega[/math]
ঘ. [math]\omega^{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
যদি, [math]p=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})=\omega^{}[/math]
 হয় তবে[math]q=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})=\omega^{2}[/math]
 হবে[math]\therefore[/math]
 [math](1+\omega)\left(1+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \left(\omega^{2}\right)(-\omega)=\omega^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #20 (ID: 439626)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]p=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math] ও [math]4=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})[/math] [math]\mathbf{p}^{3} \mathbf{q}^{5}[/math] এর মান কত হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]-\omega[/math]
খ. [math]\omega^{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\omega[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\left.\begin{array}{l}p=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3}) \\q=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})\end{array}\right\}[/math]
 এ দুটিই এককের ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math]
 ও [math]\omega^{2} \text {. }[/math]
ধরি [math]\therefore p=\omega[/math]
 এবং [math]q=\omega^{2} .[/math]
 [math]p^{3} q^{5}=\omega^{3} \cdot\left(\omega^{2}\right)^{5}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{3} \cdot \omega^{10}=\omega^{13}=\omega^{12+1}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=\omega^{12} \cdot \omega \\=\omega .\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #21 (ID: 439627)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]Z=3 \sqrt{3}-2 \sqrt{3 i}[/math] একটি জটিল রাশি।

[math]|\overline{\mathbf{Z}-\overline{\mathbf{Z}}}|[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-4 \sqrt{3} i[/math]
খ. [math]4 \sqrt{3} i[/math]
গ. [math]4 \sqrt{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-4 \sqrt{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}z=3 \sqrt{3}-2 \sqrt{3} i \\\bar{z}=3 \sqrt{3}+2 \sqrt{3} i\end{array}[/math]
 [math]z-\bar{z}=-4 \sqrt{3} i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \overline{z-\bar{z}}=1+4 \sqrt{3} i[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mid \overline{-z-\bar{i} \mid}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(4 \sqrt{3})^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=4 \sqrt{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #24 (ID: 439630)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} i[/math] হলে, [math]\mathbf{z} \overline{\mathbf{z}}[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]-\frac{5}{3}[/math]
B. [math]-\frac{3}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{5}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{z}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}+\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \mathrm{i} \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \overline{\mathbf{z}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}-\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathbf{z} \overline{\mathbf{z}}[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)^{2}-\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{3}} \mathrm{i}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{3}-\frac{4}{3}(-1)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1+4}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{5}{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #25 (ID: 439631)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে [math]|z+1|-|z-1|=0[/math] সমীকরণটি কোনটি নির্দেশ করে?

Options:
ক. x অক্ষরেখা
খ. y অক্ষরেখা
গ. বৃত্ত
ঘ. উপবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{z}[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{x}+\mathrm{iy}[/math]
 [math]\therefore z+1=(x+i y+1)[/math]
 [math]=\{(x+1)+\mathrm{iy}\}[/math]
এখন, [math]|z+1|-|z-1|=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow |z+1|=|z-1|[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x+1)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(x-1)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow (x+1)^{2}+y^{2}=[/math]
 [math](x-1)^{2}+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}+2 x+1[/math]
 [math]=x^{2}-2 x+1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow4 \mathrm{x}=0 \text { বা, } x=0[/math]
যা y অক্ষের সমীকরণ ।

Explanation:

Question #29 (ID: 439635)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1}{1+\cos \theta-i \sin \theta}[/math] এর কাল্পনিক অংশ নিম্নের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]-\frac{1}{2} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{1}{2} \cot \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 \tan \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\frac{1}{1+\cos \theta-i \sin \theta}[/math]
এখন, [math]\frac{1}{1+\cos \theta-i \sin \theta}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta}{\{(1+\cos \theta)-i \sin \theta\}\{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta\}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta}{(1+\cos \theta)^{2}-i^{2} \sin ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta}{1+2 \cos \theta+\cos ^{2} \theta+\sin ^{2} \theta}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta}{1+2 \cos \theta+1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(1+\cos \theta)+i \sin \theta}{2(1+\cos \theta)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(1+\cos \theta)}{2(1+\cos \theta)}+\frac{i \sin \theta}{2(1+\cos \theta)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{i 2 \sin \frac{\theta}{2} \cdot \cos \frac{\theta}{2}}{2 \cdot 2 \cos ^{2} \frac{\theta}{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}+i \frac{1}{2} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 কাল্পনিক অংশ [math]\frac{1}{2} \tan \frac{\theta}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে, [math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right) \times\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]2[/math]
C. [math]4[/math]
D. [math]8[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \left(0-2 \omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math](0-2 \omega)=4 \omega^{3}[/math]
 [math]=4.1=4[/math]

Question #37 (ID: 439643)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important

Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] জটিল রাশির আর্গুমেন্ট [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] হলে, এর সঞ্চারপথের সমীকরণ নিম্নের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]x+y=0[/math]
খ. [math]x+y=1[/math]
গ. [math]x-y=0[/math]
ঘ. [math]y+x=0[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
দেওয়া আছে, [math]\mathrm{z}=\mathrm{x}+\mathrm{iy}[/math]
 এর আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 শর্তানুসারে, [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}=\tan ^{-1}(\frac{y}{x})[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow\frac{\mathrm{y}}{\mathrm{x}}=\tan \left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)\\\Rightarrow\frac{\mathrm{y}}{\mathrm{x}}=-1\\\Rightarrow y=-x\\\Rightarrow x+y=0\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #43 (ID: 439587)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোন জটিল সংখ্যা ও তার অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যার সমষ্টি কিরূপ সংখ্যা?

Options:
ক. কাল্পনিক
খ. জটিল
গ. বাস্তব
ঘ. অবাস্তব


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
ধরি[math]z=a+i b[/math]
 [math]\therefore \bar{z}=a-i b[/math]
[অবাস্তব অংশের চিহ্ন বদলালে তার conjugate compex পাওয়া যায় ][math]\therefore z +\bar{z}=2 a[/math]
 যেহেতু কোনো অবাস্তব অংশ নেইতাই সমষ্টি বাস্তব হবে ।

Question #44 (ID: 439588)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{-i}[/math] = কত ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(-1-i)[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{2}(-1-i)[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1+i)[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1-i)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}-\mathrm{i} & =\frac{1}{2}(1-1-2 \mathrm{i}) \\& \Rightarrow \frac{1}{2}\left(1^{2}+\mathrm{i}^{2}-2 \cdot 1 . \mathrm{i}\right) \\& \Rightarrow \frac{1}{2}(1-i)^{2}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{-i}=\frac{\pm 1}{\sqrt{2}}(1-i)[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]x=i-2[/math] হলে [math]x^{3}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:

খ. [math]11 i+2[/math]

ঘ. [math]-11 i-2[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x^{3}=(i-2)^{3}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow i^{3}-8-3 \cdot i(i-2) \\\Rightarrow i^{2} . i-8-6 i(i-2) \\\Rightarrow -i-8-6 i^{2}+12 i \\\Rightarrow -i-8+6+12 i=11 i-2\end{array}[/math]

Question #46 (ID: 439590)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে, [math](1+\omega)\left(1+\omega^{4}\right)\left(1+\omega^{8}\right)\left(1+\omega^{12}\right)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]-2 \omega^{2}[/math]
C. [math]2 \omega^{2}[/math]
D. [math]\omega^{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}(1+\omega)\left(1+\omega^{4}\right)\left(1+\omega^{8}\right)\left(1+\omega^{12}\right) \\\Rightarrow (1+\omega)(1+\omega)\left(1+\omega^{2}\right)(1+1) \\\Rightarrow (1+\omega)^{2}\left(1+\omega^{2}\right) \cdot 2 \\\Rightarrow \left(-\omega^{2}\right)^{2} \cdot(-\omega) \cdot 2 \\\Rightarrow \omega^{4} \cdot(-\omega) \cdot 2=-2 \omega^{5}=-2 \omega^{2}\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math](\sqrt{3}-i)[/math] সংখ্যাটির পোলার আকার নিচের কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]-2\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{6}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]
খ. [math]2\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{6}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]2\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{6}-i \sin \frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{6}-i \sin \frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}r \cos \theta=\sqrt{3} ; r \sin \theta=-1 \\r=\sqrt{(-1)^{2}+(\sqrt{3})^{2}}=2 \\\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{-1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)=-\frac{\pi}{6}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 পোলার আকার :[math]2\left(\cos \frac{-\pi}{6}+i \sin \frac{-\pi}{6}\right)=2\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{6}-i \sin \frac{\pi}{6}\right)[/math]

Question:
[math]|Z+5|=x[/math] সমীকরণটির সঞ্চারপথ কোনটি?

Options:
ক. বৃত্ত
খ. উপবৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]|z+5|=x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow|x+5+i y|=x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x+5)^{2}+y^{2}}=x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}+10 x+25+y^{2}=x^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \quad y^{2}=-(10 x+25)[/math]
এটা একটা পরাবৃক্ত Parabola সমীকরণ।

বৃত্তের সমীকরণ [math](x-a)^{2}+(y-b)^{2}=r^{2}[/math]
 সাথে তুলনা করলেএটার কেন্দ্র [math]\equiv(-5 ; 0)[/math]

[math]\mathrm{Z}=\sqrt{3} \mathbf{i}+1[/math] হলে [math]\mathbf{Z} \overline{\mathrm{Z}}[/math] দ্বারা নির্দেশিত বৃত্তের ব্যাসার্ধ কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]4[/math]
C. [math]1[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}z=\sqrt{3} i+1 \\\bar{z}=-\sqrt{3} i+1\end{array}[/math]
[ conjugate complex অবাস্তব অংশের চিহ্ন বদল করে পাই ][math]\therefore[/math]
 [math]z \bar{z}[/math]
 [math]=(\sqrt{3} i+1)(-\sqrt{3} i+1)[/math]
 [math]=(\sqrt{3})^{2}-1^{2}[/math]
 [math]=3-1=2[/math]
 একক ব্যাসার্ধ

Question #54 (ID: 439598)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]1 \pm i[/math] এর বর্গমূল কিরূপ সংখ্যা?

Options:
ক. জটিল
খ. বাস্তব
গ. কাল্পনিক
ঘ. অমূলদ


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
ধরি, [math]\sqrt{1+i}=x+i y[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1+i=x^{2}+i^{2} y^{2}+2 \cdot x \cdot i y[/math]
 [math]=x^{2}-y^{2}+2 x y i[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}-y^{2}=1[/math]
 [math]\quad 2 x y=1[/math]
 [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=\sqrt{\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\left(x^{2}-y^{2}\right)^{2}+4 x^{2} y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{1+4}=\sqrt{5}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2}[/math]
 [math]y=\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{1+i}=\frac{1+\sqrt{5}}{2} \pm \frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2} i[/math]
এটি একটি জটিল সংখ্যা,

Explanation:

Question:

B. [math]0[/math]
C. [math]i[/math]
D. অসংজ্ঞায়িত


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

 [math]\Rightarrow x+i y=-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 সমাকরনের উভয় পাশে বাস্তব ও অবাস্তব অংশ সমান হবে।[math]\therefore x=-1 ; \quad y=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \frac{x}{y}=\frac{-1}{0}[/math]
 এটি অসংজ্ঞারিত

খ. [math]0[/math]
গ. [math]1[/math]
ঘ. [math]i[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{x}[/math]
 [math]=1+\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(\mathrm{x}-1)[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(x-1)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=i^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-2 x+1[/math]
 [math]=-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}-2 x+2=0[/math]
এখন, [math]x^{4}-2 x^{3}+2 x^{2}[/math]
 [math]=x^{2}\left(x^{2}-2 x+2\right)[/math]
 [math]=x^{2} \cdot 0=0[/math]

B. [math]1 \pm \mathrm{i} \sqrt{-7}[/math]

 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow y=\sqrt{-2+y} \text{.}\\\Rightarrow \mathrm{y}^{2}=-2+\mathrm{y}\\\therefore y^{2}-y+2=0\\\therefore \mathrm{y}=\frac{-(-1) \pm \sqrt{(-1)^{2}-4.2 .1}}{2.1}=\frac{1 \pm \sqrt{-7}}{2} ;=\frac{1 \pm \mathrm{i} \sqrt{7}}{2}\end{array}[/math]

 ভাগশেষ ০' হলে উত্তর [math]1[/math]
 [math]\omega^{3 k+1}=\omega\quad[/math]
 ভাগশেষ 1' হলে উত্তর [math]\omega \text {. }[/math]
 [math]\omega^{3 k+2}=\omega^{2}\quad[/math]
 ভাগশেষ 2' হলে উত্তর [math]\omega^{2}[/math]

Question #65 (ID: 439609)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
64 এর ঘনমূল হবে —

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } 4 \omega^{3}\\\text{ii. } 4 \omega\end{array}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } 4 \omega^{2}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. ii ও iii
গ. i ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]a^{3}[/math]
 যেকোনো বাস্তব সংখ্যা হলে এর তিনটি ঘনমূল [math]a, a \omega[/math]
 ও [math]a \omega^{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #66 (ID: 439610)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years:  | Boards:  | Tags: Important
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\mathbf{Z}_{1}[/math] ও [math]\mathbf{Z}_{2}[/math] জটিল সংখ্যা হলে -

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } \overline{\mathrm{z}_{1}+\mathrm{z}_{2}}=\overline{\mathrm{z}_{1}}+\overline{\mathrm{z}_{2}}\\\text{ii. } \left|z_{1}\right|\left|z_{2}\right|=\left|z_{1} z_{2}\right|\end{array}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } \overline{\mathrm{z}_{1} \mathrm{z}_{2}}=\overline{\mathrm{z}_{1}} \overline{\mathrm{z}_{2}}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
ধরি,[math]\begin{array}{l}-2_{1}=a+i b \\z_{2}=c+i d\end{array}[/math]
অতএব[math]\begin{array}{l}\bar{z}_{1}=a-i b \\\bar{z}_{2}=c-i d\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\text { (i) } \quad-z_{1}+z_{2}=(a+c)+i(b+d) \\L S=\overline{z_{1}+z_{2}}=(a+c)-i(b+d) \\\end{array}[/math]
 [math]R S =\bar{z}_{1}+\bar{z}_{2}[/math]
 [math]=a-i b+c-i d[/math]
 [math]=a+c-i(b+d)[/math]
 [math]\therefore L S=R S[/math]
 [math]\text { (ii) }[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\left|z_{1}\right|=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}} \\\left|z_{2}\right|=\sqrt{c^{2}+d^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore L S=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}} \sqrt{c^{2}+d^{2}}[/math]
 [math]R S=|a c-b d+i(a d+b c)|[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(a c-b d)^{2}+(a d+b c)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\left(a^{2}+b^{2}\right)\left(c^{2}+d^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\therefore L S=R S .[/math]
 [math]\text { (iii) }[/math]
 [math]L S=z_{1} z_{2}=a c-b d+i b c+i a d \text {. }[/math]
 [math]R S=\bar{z}_{1} \bar{z}_{2}=a c+b d-i b c-i a d[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad \text { LS } 
eq \text { RS }[/math]


Explanation:

Question #67 (ID: 439611)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে—

[math]\text { i. } \quad 1=-\omega-\omega^{2}[/math] [math]\text { ii. } \omega^{3 n+2}=\omega^{2} ; n \in \mathbb{N} \text {. }[/math] [math]\text { iii. } \quad \omega^{n}=(\omega)[/math] যদি [math]n[/math] কে [math]3[/math] দ্বারা ভাগ করলে ভাগশেষ [math]2[/math] হয়

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
ক. i
খ. i ও ii
গ. i ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]i[/math]
 আমরা জানি [math]1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 1=-\omega-\omega^{2}[/math]
(ii) আমরা জানি [math]\omega^{3 n}=1=[/math]
 [math]\omega^{3 n+2}=\omega^{3 n} \cdot \omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=1 \cdot \omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2}[/math]
(iii) [math]\omega^{n}=\omega[/math]
 হবে যখন [math]n=3 k+1[/math]
অতএব 3 দ্বারা ভাগ করলে ভাগশেষ 1 থাকবে

Explanation:

Question:
দুটি অনুবন্ধী জটিল সংখ্যার গুণফল-

i. জটিল সংখ্যা

ii. বাস্তব সংখ্যা

iii. ধনাত্মক সংখ্যা

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: গ

 [math]z \bar{z}=(a+i b)(a-i b)[/math]
 [math]=a^{2}+i a b-i a b-i^{2} b^{2}[/math]
 [math]=a^{2}+b^{2}[/math]
 যেটা একটা বাস্তাব ও ধন্যাত্মক সংখ্যা

Question #71 (ID: 439572)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
(1 + i) এর মডুলাস এবং আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. 2, 135°
খ. 2, -135°
গ. √2, 135°
ঘ. √2,-135°


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]Hints: - a + ib[/math]
 এর মডুলাস [math]r=\sqrt{(-a)^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]
দ্বিতীয় চর্তুভাগে অবস্থিত হওয়ায় [math]\text { Argument, } \theta=180^{\circ}-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{y}{-x}\right|[/math]
 [math]Solve : (-1+i)[/math]
 এর মডুলাস [math]r=\sqrt{(-1)^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]
আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=180-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{-1}\right|=135^{\circ}[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা: মডুলাস = [math]\sqrt{}[/math]
 ( বাস্তব অংশ [math]^2[/math]
 + কাল্পনিক অংশ [math]^2[/math]
 )আর্গুমেন্ট: x + iy আকারের জটিল সংখ্যার আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{x}\right)[/math]
- x + iy আকারের জটিল সংখ্যার আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=\pi-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{y}{-x}\right|[/math]
- x - iy আকারের জটিল সংখ্যার আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{-y}{-x}\right| \pm \pi[/math]
x - iy আকারের জটিল সংখ্যার আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{-y}{x}\right|[/math]

Question #72 (ID: 439573)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
একটি ক্লাসে 16 টি ছেলের ব্যাট, 11 টি ছেলের বল এবং 6 টি ছেলের একইসাথে ব্যাট ও বল আছে। কতজন ছেলের হয় ব্যাট না হয় বল আছে?

Options:
ক. 5
খ. 10
গ. 12
ঘ. 15


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
Hints: যাদের ব্যাট ও বল উভয়ই আছে তাদেরকে বাদ দিতে হবে।Solve: শুধু ব্যাট আছে [math]= 16-6 = 10[/math]
 জন শুধু বল আছে [math]= 11 - 6 = 5[/math]
 জন.:. ব্যাট না হয় (অথবা) বল আছে [math]= 10 + 5 = 15[/math]
 জন

Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা: ব্যাট না হয় বল আছে বলতে বুঝাচ্ছে একজন ছেলের ব্যাট অথবা বল যে কোনো একটা আছে। ফলে প্রথমে যাদের শুধু ব্যাট আছে তাদের সংখ্যা বের করা হয়েছে। এক্ষেত্রে মোট ব্যাট থাকা ছেলেদের থেকে ব্যাট ও বল উভয় থাকা ছেলেদের বাদ দেওয়া হয়েছে। একইভাবে যাদের শুধু বল আছে তাদের সংখ্যা বের করা হয়েছে। এরপর উভয়কে যোগ করা হয়েছে।

Question #73 (ID: 439574)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2009-2010 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোনটি [math]x^ 3 = 8[/math] এর সমাধান নয়?

Options:
ক. [math]0[/math]
খ. [math]-1+ i√3[/math]
গ. [math]-1- i√3[/math]
ঘ. [math]√2 (1+i)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
Hints: [math]x^3 = 8[/math]
 সমীকরণকে সমাধান করতে হবে।[math]x^{3}=8 \Rightarrow x^{3}-8=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{3}-2^{3}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(x-2)[/math]
 [math]\left(x^{2}+2 x+4\right)[/math]
 [math]=0 \Rightarrow x=2[/math]
 অথবা [math]x^{2}+2 x+4=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{4-16}}{2.1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{-12}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-2 \pm \sqrt{12 \mathrm{i}^{2}}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-2 \pm 2 \mathrm{i} \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=-1 \pm \mathrm{i} \sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]=-1+\mathrm{i} \sqrt{3}[/math]
 এবং [math]-1-\mathrm{i} \sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}[/math]
 [math]=2,-1+\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{3},-1-\mathrm{i} \sqrt{3}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #75 (ID: 439576)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2008-2009 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\left(\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}\right)+\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}\right)=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]-1[/math]
C. [math]2 \omega[/math]
D. [math]2 \omega^{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\text { Hints: } \frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}=\omega, \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}=\omega^{2}[/math]
Solve: আমরা জানি, [math]\left(\omega+\omega^{2}+1=0 \\\therefore\omega+\omega^{2}=-1\right.[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যাঃ 1 এর ঘনমূলের মধ্যে অবাস্তব ঘনমূল দুটি হচ্ছে [math]\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2} , \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2} ;[/math]
যাদে থাক্রমে ω ও ω^2 দ্বারা সূচিত করা হয়।[math]\left(\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}\right)+\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}\right)=\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}=\frac{-2}{2}=-1[/math]
অর্থাৎ [math]\omega+\omega^{2}=-1 \Rightarrow \omega+\omega^{2}+1=0[/math]

Question #76 (ID: 439577)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোনটি [math]x³ = 1[/math] এর সমাধান নয়?

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math](-1-i \sqrt{3}) / 2[/math]
গ. [math](-1 + i√3)/2[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
ঙ. [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: E. [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{3}[/math]

Solution:
1 এর ঘনমূল অর্থাৎ x³ = 1 এর সমাধানগুলি হচ্ছে[math]1, \frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}, \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math]
আবার, অপশন (D) থেকে [math]\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{2 \pi}{3}=\cos 120^{\circ}+i \sin 120^{\circ}[/math]
 [math]=-\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}[/math]
অর্থাৎ অপশন (D) ও হচ্ছে সমীকরণটির একটি সমাধান।কিন্তু অপশন (E) থেকে [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\cos 60^{\circ}+i \sin 60^{\circ}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}+\mathrm{i} \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math]
 এটি 1 এর ঘনমূল নয়

Explanation:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text { } x^{3}=1 \Rightarrow x^{3}-1=0 \Rightarrow(x-1)\left(x^{2}+x+1\right)=0 \Rightarrow x=1 \\\text { Or, } x^{2}+x+1=0 \Rightarrow x=\frac{(-1) \pm \sqrt{(-1)^{2}-4.1 .1}}{2.1} \\=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1-4}}{2}=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{3} i}{2}\end{array}[/math]
অর্থাৎ x³ = 1 এর সমাধানগুলি হচ্ছে [math]1, \frac{-1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}, \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\ \\ \\ \\ \ \\[/math]
By Calculator: ক্যালকুলেটরকে COMPLEX মুডে রেখে Option গুলোকে cube করতে পারো। যেগুলোর cube সমান 1 আসবে, সেগুলোই x³ = 1 এর সমাধান। (জটিল সংখ্যা। কে ক্যালকুলেটরে input 3 দেওয়ার জন্য Shift ও ENG বাটন চাপতে হবে)

Question:
[math]\mathbf{z}_{1}=3+2 \mathbf{i}[/math] এবং [math]z_{2}=3-2 \mathbf{i}[/math] হলে, [math]\mathbf{z}_{1}^{2}+\mathbf{z}_{1} \mathbf{z}_{2}+\mathbf{z}_{2}^{2}[/math] মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]21[/math]
B. [math]22[/math]
C. [math]23[/math]
D. [math]24[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{z}_{1}^{2}+\mathrm{z}_{1}\mathrm{z}_{2}+\mathrm{z}_{2}^{2}=\left(\mathrm{z}_{1}+\mathrm{z}_{2}\right)^{2}-\mathrm{z}_{1} \mathrm{z}_{2}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(3+2 i+3-2 i)^{2}-(3+2 i)(3-2 i) \\=6^{2}-\left(3^{2}+2^{2}\right)=36-13=23\end{array}[/math]

Question #79 (ID: 439580)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012, 2005-2006 | Boards: Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board, Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোন দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণের একটি মূল [math]1+\sqrt{-7}[/math] হলে সমীকরণটি কত?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+2 x+8=0[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-2 x+8=0[/math]
গ. [math]x^{2}-2 x-8=0[/math]
ঘ. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
একটি মূল [math]1+\sqrt{-7}[/math]
 সুতরাং অপরটি [math]1-\sqrt{-7}[/math]
 [math]x^{2}-x[/math]

Options:

ঘ. [math]\pm(1-2 \mathrm{i})[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]-2 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]=1^{2}+21[/math]
 [math](-\mathrm{i})+(-\mathrm{i})^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(1-\mathrm{i})^{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{-2 \mathrm{i}}=\pm(1-\mathrm{i})[/math]

Question #83 (ID: 439562)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2011-2012, 2010-2011, 2007-2008 | Boards:  | Tags: Jagannath University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{i}=\sqrt{\frac{2i}{2}}=\sqrt{\frac{(1+i)^2}{2}}=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}};\sqrt{i}=\frac{1-i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}=\frac{1+i+1-i}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #84 (ID: 439563)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2013-2014, 2006-2007, 2005-2006 | Boards: Sylhet Board, Sylhet Board | Tags: Sylhet Board, Jhenidah Government Veterinary College, Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University, Sylhet Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]2i[/math] এর বর্গমূল কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]-1+i[/math]
খ. [math]1-i[/math]
গ. [math]-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm(1+i)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]2 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]=1+2 \mathrm{i}+\mathrm{i}^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(1+\mathrm{i})^{2}[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \mathrm{i}^{2}=-1\right] \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{2 \mathrm{i}}[/math]
 [math]=\pm(1+\mathrm{i})[/math]


Explanation:

Question #88 (ID: 439567)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012, 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\mathrm{x}=-1+\mathrm{i}[/math] হয় তবে [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}+4 x+7[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]-5[/math]
B. [math]-3[/math]
C. [math]5[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}+4 x+7[/math]
 [math]=(-1+i)^{3}[/math]
 [math]+3(-1+i)^{2}[/math]
 [math]+4(-1+i)+7)[/math]
 [math]=-1-i+3+3 i[/math]
 [math]+3(1-1-2 i)[/math]
 [math]-4+4 i+7[/math]
 [math]=5[/math]


Explanation:

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]-2-2 i[/math] জটিল রাশিটির আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]-2-2 i ; \tan \theta \frac{y}{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{y}{x}\right)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{-2}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}(1)=\tan ^{-1}\left(\tan \frac{\pi}{4}\right)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-\mathrm{i}}}{\sqrt{2}}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]i \sqrt{2}[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{-2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{(\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i})^{2}}}{\sqrt{2}} ; \frac{\sqrt{i-i+2 \sqrt{-i^{2}}}}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{2}}{\sqrt{2}}=1[/math]

Question #107 (ID: 439550)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোনটি [math]x^{3}=1[/math] এর সমাধান নয়?

Options:
ক. 1
খ. [math](-1-i \sqrt{3}) / 2[/math]
গ. [math](-1+i \sqrt{3}) / 2[/math]
ঘ. [math]\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
ঙ. [math]\cos \frac{ \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{ \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: E. [math]\cos \frac{ \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{ \pi}{3}[/math]

Solution:
Hints: [math]x^{3}=1[/math]
 এর সমাধান সেগুলোই, যেগুলো 1 এর ঘনমূল ৷Solve: 1 এর ঘনমূল অর্থাৎ [math]x^{3}=1[/math]
 এর সমাধানগুলি হচ্ছে:[math]1, \frac{-1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}, \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}[/math]
 আবার, অপশন (D) থেকে [math]\cos \frac{2 \pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]=\cos 120^{\circ}+i \sin 120^{\circ}=-\frac{1}{2}+i \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math]
অর্থাৎ অপশন (D)-ও হচ্ছে সমীকরণটির একটি সমাধান। কিন্তু অপশন (E) থেকে [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{3}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{3}=\cos 60^{\circ}+i \sin 60^{\circ}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}+i \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}=\frac{1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math]
 এটি 1 এর ঘনমূল নয়।

Explanation:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\text {} x^{3}=1 \Rightarrow x^{3}-1=0 \Rightarrow(x-1)\left(x^{2}+x+1\right)=0 \\x=1 \text { Or, } x^{2}+x+1=0 \Rightarrow x=\frac{(-1) \pm \sqrt{(-1)^{2}-4 \cdot 1 \cdot 1}}{2.1} \\=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1-4}}{2}=\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{3} i}{2}\end{array}[/math]
অর্থাৎ [math]x^{3}=1[/math]
 এর সমাধানগুলি হচ্ছে [math]1, \frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}, \frac{-1-\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math]

Question:
[math]i^{4n+3}[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]i[/math]
খ. [math]-i[/math]
গ. [math]-1[/math]
ঘ. None of these


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]i^{4n+3}=i^{4n}.i^3=-i[/math]

Question #111 (ID: 439554)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[4]{-16}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]
B. [math]\pm(\sqrt{3} \pm i)[/math]
C. [math]\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]
D. [math]\pm \sqrt{2}(1 \pm i)[/math]
E. [math]\frac{1}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt[4]{-16}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt[4]{16 i^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\pm 4 i}[/math]
 [math]=2 \sqrt{\pm i}[/math]
 [math]=2 \sqrt{\frac{1}{2} \cdot(\pm 2 i)}[/math]
 [math]=2 \sqrt{\frac{1}{2}(1 \pm i)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\pm 2 \cdot \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]
 [math]=\pm \sqrt{2}(1 \pm i)[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]1+\sqrt{3} i[/math] এর মডুলাস ও আর্গুমেন্ট এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]2[/math] [math]\&[/math] [math]\pi /3[/math]
খ. [math]3[/math] [math]\&[/math] [math]\pi /2[/math]
গ. [math]2[/math] [math]\&[/math] [math]\pi /2[/math]
ঘ. [math]3[/math] [math]\&[/math] [math]\pi /3[/math]
ঙ. [math]1[/math] [math]\&[/math] [math]\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]r=\sqrt{1^{2}+3}=2 ; \tan \theta=\frac{\sqrt{3}}{1} \quad \therefore \theta=\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{3}=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]

Question #113 (ID: 439556)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2006-2007 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math](1-\omega)\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)\left(1-\omega^{4}\right)\left(1-\omega^{8}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]18[/math]
B. [math]6[/math]
C. [math]-9[/math]
D. [math]9[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{8}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math](1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left\{(1-\omega)\left(1-\omega^2\right)\right\}^2[/math]
 [math]=\left(1-\omega-\omega^2+\omega^3\right)^2[/math]
 [math]=(1+1+1)^2[/math]
 [math]=9[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i})^2[/math]
 [math]=i-i+2\sqrt{-i^2}=2\quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{2.}[/math]

Question #116 (ID: 439456)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1-i x}{1+i x}=a-i b[/math] এবং [math]x,a[/math] ও [math]b[/math] বাস্তব হলে, নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. [math]a^{2}+b^{2}=1[/math]
খ. [math]a^{2}-b^{2}=1[/math]
গ. [math]a^{2}+b^{2}=-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]a^{2}+b^{2}=0[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\frac{1-ix}{1+ix}=a-ib\ \\\Rightarrow\left|\frac{1+ix}{1-ix}\right|=|a-ib|\\\Rightarrow\frac{|1+ix|}{|1-ix\mid}=|a-ib|[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{\sqrt{1^{2}+1^{2}}}{\sqrt{1^{2}+(-1)^{2}}}=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}} \therefore a^{2}+b^{2}=1[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #117 (ID: 439457)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x^{4}=1[/math] সমীকরণটির মূলগুলির বর্গের সমষ্টি কত?

Options:
ক. [math]1+i[/math]
খ. [math]1-i[/math]
গ. [math]0[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x^{4}=1 \\\Rightarrow x^{2}=\pm 1[/math]
 [বর্গমূল করে]এখন, [math]x^{2}=1[/math]
 অথবা, [math]\mathrm{x}^{2}=(-1)=\mathrm{i}^{2}[/math]
 [বর্গমূল করে] বা, [math]x=\pm 1[/math]
বা, [math]x^{2}=i^{2}[/math]
 [বর্গমূল করে]তাহলে, নির্নেয় মূলগুলো হল [math]=1,(-1), \mathrm{i},(-\mathrm{i})[/math]
সুতরাং,মূলগুলোর বর্গের সমষ্টি [math]=1^{2}+(-1)^{2}+i^{2}+(-i)^{2}=(2-2)=0[/math]


Explanation:
[math]x^{4}=1[/math]
 সমীকরণটি সমাধান করা আর 1 এর চতুর্মূলগুলি বের করা কিংবা [math]\sqrt[4]{1}[/math]
 এর মান নির্ণয় করা—সবই আসলে একই জিনিস। এ অংকের ক্ষেত্রে তা-ই করা হয়েছে। । এর চারটি মূল বের করে সেগুলোকে বা করে। manually যোগ করা হয়েছে।

Question #118 (ID: 439458)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]q[/math] এর কোন মূলদ মানের জন্য [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}-27 x+q=0[/math] সমীকরণটির একটি মূল [math]2+\sqrt{3}[/math] হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]2[/math]
খ. [math]3[/math]
গ. [math]-4[/math]
ঘ. [math]-5[/math]
ঙ. [math]7[/math]


Correct Answer: E.[math]7[/math]

Solution:
[math]q[/math]
 এর মূলদ মানের জন্য প্রদত্ত সমীকরণটি হবে মূলদ সহগবিশিষ্ট সমীকরণ। এর ফলে, এই সমীকরণের অমূলদ মূলগুলো অনুবন্ধী জোড় আকারে আসবে। একটি মূল [math](2+\sqrt{3})[/math]
 হলে, আরেকটি মূল [math](2-\sqrt{3})[/math]
 হবে, এখন [math](2+\sqrt{3})[/math]
 এবং [math](2-\sqrt{3})[/math]
 মূলবিশিষ্ট সমীকরণ হচ্ছে, [math]x^{2}-4 x+1=0[/math]
তাহলে, [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}-27 x+q=0 \text { or, } x\left(x^{2}-4 x+1\right)+7\left(x^{2}-4 x+1\right)+q-7=0[/math]
 [math]\text { or, } \mathrm{x} \times 0+7 \times 0+\mathrm{q}-7=0 \quad \therefore \mathrm{q}=7[/math]
 [মান বসিয়ে]

Explanation:

Question #119 (ID: 439459)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2007-2008, 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology, Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]-8-6 \sqrt{-1}[/math] এর বর্গমূল হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]\pm(1+3 i)[/math]
খ. [math]\pm(1-3 i)[/math]
গ. [math]3-i[/math]
ঘ. [math]3+i[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\pm\sqrt{-8-6i}[/math]
 [math]=\pm\sqrt{1^2-2.3.i.1+(3i)^2}[/math]
 [math]=\pm\sqrt{(1-3i)^2}[/math]
 [math]=\pm(1-3i)[/math]
Alternate: [math]\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\{\sqrt{r+a}\pm i\sqrt{r-a}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left\{\sqrt{10-8}\pm i\sqrt{10+8}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\pm(1-3i)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #124 (ID: 439464)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে [math]|z-2|=5[/math] সমীকরণটি নির্দেশ করে-

Options:
ক. একজোড়া সরলরেখা
খ. একটি বিন্দু
গ. একটি বৃত্ত
ঘ. একটি পরাবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]|(x-2)+i y|=5 \quad \therefore(x-2)^{2}+y^{2}=5^{2} \quad \therefore[/math] বৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Explanation:

Question #125 (ID: 439465)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]9+40 i[/math] এর বর্গমূল কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\pm(3+4 i)[/math]
খ. [math]\pm(3-4 i)[/math]
গ. [math]\pm(5+4 i)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm(4+5 i)[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math](9+40 \mathrm{i})[/math]
 [math]=(5)^{2}+2.5 .4 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]+(4 \mathrm{i})^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(5+4 \mathrm{i})^{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{9+40 \mathrm{i}}=\pm(5+4 \mathrm{i})[/math]

Question #126 (ID: 439466)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{5-i}{2-3i}[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{2\pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\frac{5-i}{2-3i}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(5-i)(2+3i)}{4+9}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{13}(10+15i-2i+3)[/math]
 [math]=1+i[/math]
 [math]\rightarrow\arg^{-1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan^{-1}\frac{1}{1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #128 (ID: 439468)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]A=\frac{a+ia}{b-ic}+id[/math] সমীকরণে [math]a,b,c,[/math] ও [math]d[/math] বাস্তব ধনাত্বক সংখ্যা এবং শূন্যের চেয়ে বড়। [math]c>b[/math] হলে [math]A[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট [math]\ \theta=?[/math]

Options:
ক. [math].0<\theta<90^{\circ}[/math]
খ. [math]90^{\circ}<\theta{ }<180^{\circ}[/math]
গ. [math]180^{\circ}<\theta<270^{\circ}[/math]
ঘ. [math]270^{\circ}<\theta<360^{\circ}[/math]
ঙ. [math]-90^{\circ}<\theta<0^{\circ}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]A=\frac{a+ia}{b-ic}+id[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(a+b)(b+ic)}{b^2+c^2}+id[/math]
 [math]=\frac{a(b-c)}{b^2+c^2}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{ba(b+c)}{b^2+c^2}+id[/math]
 [math]=\frac{a(b-c)}{b^2+c^2}+i\frac{a(b+c)+d\left(b^2+c^2\right)}{b^2+c^2}[/math]
 [math]\theta=[/math]
 [math]\arg A=\tan ^{-1}[/math]
 [math]\frac{a(b+c)+d\left(b^{2}+c^{2}\right)}{a(b-c)}[/math]
যেহেতু [math]a, b, c, d>0[/math]
 এবং [math]c>b[/math]
 তাই লব ধনাত্বক কিন্তু হর ঋনাত্বক। আবার, [math]tan[/math]
 অনুপাত ঋনাত্বক হয় ২য় বা ৪র্থ চতুর্ভাগে।[math]\therefore90^{\circ}<\theta<180^{\circ}[/math]
 ই সঠিক উত্তর। [math]\left[\tan^{-1}\frac{y}{-x}\pi-\tan^{-1}\frac{y}{x}\right][/math]


Explanation:

Question #132 (ID: 439472)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]i[/math]-এর বর্গমূল কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\pm \frac{\sqrt{2}}{1-i}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1-i)[/math]
গ. [math]\pm \frac{\sqrt{2}}{1+i}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm \sqrt{2}(1-i)[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\frac{(1+0)^2}{2}=\frac{1+2i-1}{2}=\frac{2i}{2}=\mathrm{i}\therefore\mathrm{i}=\frac{(1+i)^2}{2}\\\Rightarrow\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}=\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1+\mathrm{i})=\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\frac{2}{1-i}=\pm\frac{\sqrt{2}}{1-i}[/math]

Question #133 (ID: 439473)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{i}=\sqrt{\frac{2i}{2}}=\sqrt{\frac{(1+i)^2}{2}}=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}};\sqrt{i}=\frac{1-i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}=\frac{1+i+1-i}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #136 (ID: 439476)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[3]{x+i y}=p+iq[/math] হলে কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. [math]4\left(p^{2}+q^{2}\right)=\frac{\pi}{p}+\frac{y}{q}[/math]
খ. [math]4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)=\frac{x}{p}+\frac{y}{q}[/math]
গ. [math]4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)=\frac{x}{p}-\frac{y}{q}[/math]
ঘ. [math]4\left(p^{2}+q^{2}\right)=\frac{x}{p^{2}}+\frac{y}{q^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt[3]{x+iy}=p+iq[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x+iy=p^3+3p^2[/math]
 [math].iq-3pq^2-iq^3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\ x+iy=\left(p^3-3pq^2\right)+[/math]
 [math]i\left(3p^2q-q^3\right)\quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=p^3-3pq^2[/math]
 [math]y=3p^2q-q^3\[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\frac{x}{p}=p^2-3q^2[/math]
 [math]\ldots\ldots\text{ (i) }[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\frac{y}{q}=3p^2-q^2[/math]
 [math]\ldots\ldots\left(ii\right)[/math]
 [math](i)[/math]
 ও [math](ii)[/math]
 যোগ করে, [math]\frac{x}{p}+\frac{y}{q}[/math]
 [math]=4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #137 (ID: 439477)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x=-1+i[/math] হলে [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}+4 x+7[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]6+i[/math]
খ. [math]8[/math]
গ. [math]5[/math]
ঘ. [math]9+3i[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{x}=-1+\mathrm{i};\mathrm{\ x}^2=-2\mathrm{i};\mathrm{\ x}^3=2+2\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\therefore\mathrm{x}^3+3\mathrm{x}^2+4\mathrm{x}+7[/math]
 [math]=(2+2\mathrm{i})+3(-2\mathrm{i})+4\left(-1+i\right)+7[/math]
 [math]=2+2i-6i-4+4i+7[/math]
 [math]=5[/math]


Explanation:

 [math]\mathrm{Z}_{2}=3+\mathrm{i}[/math]
 এর [math]r_{2}=\sqrt{3^{2}+1^{2}}=\sqrt{10}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{Z}_{1} \mathrm{Z}_{2}[/math]
 এর [math]R=\sqrt{5}.\sqrt{10}=\sqrt{50}=5\sqrt{2}[/math]

Question #140 (ID: 439480)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015 | Boards:  | Tags: Sher-e-Bangla Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[4]{-81}[/math] এর মান কত ?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{9}{2}(\pm 2 \mathrm{i})[/math]
খ. [math]\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}} \sqrt{\pm 2 i}[/math]
গ. [math]\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm \frac{3}{2}(1 \pm i)[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x=\sqrt[4]{-81} \Rightarrow x^{4}=-81[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(x^{2}\right)^{2}=(i 9)^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(x^{2}\right)^{2}-(i 9)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(x^{2}+i 9\right)\left(x^{2}-i 9\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}=i 9[/math]
 এবং [math]x^{2}=-i 9[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}=\frac{9}{2}(2 i)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{9}{2}\left(1+2 i+i^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{9}{2}(1+i)^{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}(1+i)[/math]
এবং [math]x^{2}=\frac{9}{2}(-2 i)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{9}{2}\left(1-2 i+i^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{9}{2}(1-i)^{2} \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}(1-i)[/math]
 একত্রে [math]x=\pm \frac{3}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]


Explanation:

 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(3+2 i+3-2 i)^{2}-(3+2 i)(3-2 i) \\=6^{2}-\left(3^{2}+2^{2}\right)=36-13=23\end{array}[/math]

Question #148 (ID: 439488)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]|x-1+i y|+|x+1+i y|=4[/math] দ্বারা প্রকাশ করা যায় একটি বক্ররেখা-

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=7[/math]
খ. [math]y^{2}=4 x[/math]
গ. [math]y^{2}=x^{2}+1[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{x^{2}}{4}+\frac{y^{2}}{3}=1[/math]
ঙ. None


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]|x-1+i y|+|x+1+i y|=4[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x-1)^{2}+y^{2}}+[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{(x+1)^{2}+y^{2}}=4[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(x-1)^{2}+y^{2}=16-8[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{(x+1)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]+(x+1)^{2}+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 8 \sqrt{x+1)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=16+[/math]
 [math]\left[(x+1)^{2}-(x-1)^{2}\right][/math]
 [math]=16+4 x=4(4+x)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4(x+1)^{2}+4 y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(x+4)^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 4 x^{2}+8 x+4+4[/math]
 [math]y^{2}=x^{2}+8 x+16[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 3 x^{2}+4 y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=12 \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{x^{2}}{4}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{y^{2}}{3}=1.[/math]


Explanation:

[math]8+4 \sqrt{5 i}[/math] এর বর্গমূল নির্ণয় কর।

Options:

খ. [math]\pm(\sqrt{10}-\sqrt{2} i)[/math]
গ. [math]\pm(\sqrt{10}+\sqrt{2} i)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm(3+2 i)[/math]
ঙ. None


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]8+4\sqrt{5}i[/math]
 [math]=10+2\sqrt{10}\sqrt{2}i-2[/math]
 [math]=(\sqrt{10})^2+2.[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{10}[/math]
 [math].\sqrt{2}i+(\sqrt{2}i)^2[/math]
 [math]=(\sqrt{10}+\sqrt{2}i)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{8+4 \sqrt{5} i}[/math]
 [math]=\pm(\sqrt{10}+\sqrt{2} i)[/math]

B. [math]\frac{5 \sqrt{65}}{13}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\sqrt{11}}{9}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\sqrt{29}}{7}[/math]
E. [math]\frac{\sqrt{39}}{11}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

 [math]=\left|\frac{(2+i)^{3}}{2+3 i}\right|[/math]
 [math]=\frac{|2+11 i|}{|2+3 i|}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\sqrt{4+121}}{\sqrt{4+9}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{5 \sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{13}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{5 \sqrt{65}}{13}[/math]

Question #152 (ID: 439492)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে, [math]|z-8|+|z+8|=20[/math] দ্বারা নির্দেশিত সঞ্চারপথটি কি হবে?

Options:
ক. বৃত্ত
খ. উপবৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. সরলরেখা


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]z=x+i y[/math]
 [math]|x+i y-8|[/math]
 [math]+|x+i y+8|[/math]
 [math]=20 \text { Or, }[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{(x-8)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]+\sqrt{\left.(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)}=20[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } \left.(x-8)^{2}+y^{2}=400-40 \sqrt{\left.(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)}+(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, }-32 x=400-40[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{\left.(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\quad \text { Or, }-4 x=50-5 \sqrt{\left.(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } 4 x+50=5[/math]
 [math]\sqrt{\left.(x+8)^{2}+y^{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\quad \text { Or, }(4 x+50)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=25(x+8)^{2}+25 y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } 16 x^{2}[/math]
 [math]+400 x+2500[/math]
 [math]=25 x^{2}+400 x+[/math]
 [math]1600+25 y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } 9 x^{2}+25 y^{2}=900 \quad[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } \frac{x^{2}}{100}+\frac{y^{2}}{36}=1[/math]
যা একটি উপবৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Explanation:

Question #154 (ID: 439494)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)\left(\omega+\omega^{2}-1\right)\left(\omega^{2}+1-\omega\right)[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]8[/math]
B. [math]-8[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(\omega+\omega^{2}-1\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(\omega^{2}+1-\omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0 \\\omega+\omega^{2}=-1 \\1+\omega=-\omega^{2} \\\omega^{2}+1=-\omega[/math]
 [math]=\left(-\omega^{2}-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math](-1-1)(-\omega-\omega)[/math]
 [math]=-2 \omega^{2} \times 2 \times 2 \omega[/math]
 [math]=-8 \omega^{3}=-8[/math]


Explanation:

Question #155 (ID: 439495)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]i[/math]- এর ঘনমূলগুলো হল-

Options:
ক. [math]-\mathrm{i}, \frac{\mathrm{i} \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]-i, \frac{-i \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{i}, \frac{\mathrm{i} \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\mathrm{i}, \frac{-\mathrm{i} \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x^{3}=-i \quad[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } x^{3}+i[/math]
 [math]=0 \quad[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } x^{3}-(i)^{3}=0 \quad[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, }(x-i)\left(x^{2}+i x-1\right)=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{i} \quad[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{ix}-1[/math]
 [math]=0 \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=\frac{-\mathrm{i} \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]

Options:
ক. পরাবৃত্ত
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. উপবৃত্ত
ঘ. সরলরেখা


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:

 [math]\sqrt{(x-2)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(x-3)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, }[/math]
 [math]x^{2}-2 x+4+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=x^{2}-6 x+9+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, } 4[/math]
 [math]x=5[/math]
 [math]\text { Or, }[/math]
 [math]x+\frac{5}{4}[/math]
 একটি সরলরেখার সঞ্চারপথ।

Question:

Options:
ক. [math]2[/math]
খ. [math]3[/math]
গ. [math]5[/math]
ঘ. [math]4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]|z-5|=3 \\\Rightarrow|x-5+i y|=3 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x-5)^{2}+y^{2}}=3 \therefore(x-5)^{2}+y^{2}=(3)^{2}[/math]
এটি [math](x-\alpha)^{2}+(y-\beta)^{2}=r^{2}[/math]
 এর মত,যেখানে [math]r=3[/math]

Options:
ক. [math]\pm 2 i[/math]
খ. [math]\left(\sqrt{3} \pm i^{2}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]\sqrt{2 i}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:

 [math]\Rightarrow x^{6}=(8 i)^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{3}=(\pm 2 i)^{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(\frac{x}{\pm 2 i}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{x}{\pm 2i}=1, \omega, \omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\pm 2 i, \pm 2 i \omega,[/math]
 [math]\pm 2 i \omega^{2}[/math]

Question:
কোনো দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণের একটি মূল [math]1+\sqrt{-7}[/math] হলে সমীকরণ কি হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}-2 x+8=0[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-2 x-8=0[/math]
গ. [math]x^{2}+2 x-8=0[/math]
ঘ. কোনটিই নয়


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
একটি দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণের একটি মূল, [math]1+\sqrt{-7}=1+\sqrt{7} i[/math]
 হলে অপর মূলটি হবে [math]1-\sqrt{7} i[/math]
নির্ণেয় সমীকরণ, [math]x^{2}-(1+\sqrt{7} i+1-\sqrt{7} i)[/math]
 [math]x+(1+\sqrt{7} i)[/math]
 [math](1-\sqrt{7} i)[/math]
 [math]=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-2 x+1[/math]
 [math]-(\sqrt{7} i)^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-2 x+8=0[/math]


Explanation:

Question #165 (ID: 439505)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2013-2014 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]a=b^{2}[/math] এবং [math]\mathrm{b}=\mathrm{a}^{2}[/math] হয় যেখানে [math]a 
eq b,[/math] তাহলে কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. a+b=0
খ. a-b=0
গ. a+b+1=0
ঘ. a+b-1=0
ঙ. a-b-1=0


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
Hints: [math]\omega+\omega^{2}+1=0[/math]
 and [math]\omega^{3}=1[/math]
Solve: [math]a=b^{2}[/math]
 এবং [math]\mathrm{b}=\mathrm{a}^{2}[/math]
, যেখানে [math]a 
eq b[/math]
; এটা a ও b এর কোনো বাস্তব মানের জন্য সম্ভব নয়।[math]a=\omega[/math]
 এবং [math]b=\omega^{2}[/math]
 হলে সম্পর্কদ্বয় সত্য হয় ।আবার ω এর ক্ষেত্রে আমরা জানি, [math]\omega+\omega^{2}+1=0[/math]
 [math]a+b+1=0\left[\because \omega=a, \omega^{2}=b\right][/math]


Explanation:

Question #166 (ID: 439506)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math](2 \sqrt{3}-2 i)(-2 \sqrt{3}+6 i)[/math] এর পোলার আকার হলো-

Options:
ক. [math]16\sqrt{3}e^{i\pi/2}[/math]
খ. [math]16\sqrt{3}e^{3i\pi/2}[/math]
গ. [math]16\sqrt{3}e^{i\pi/4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]16\sqrt{3}e^{3i\pi/4}[/math]
ঙ. [math]16\sqrt{3}e^{5i\pi/4}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]a+ib=r(\cos\theta+i\sin\theta)[/math]
 হলে [math]a+ib=re^{i\theta}[/math]
[অয়লারের সুত্র][math]2 \sqrt{3}-2 i[/math]
 এর মডুলাস, [math]r_{1}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(2 \sqrt{3})^{2}+(-2)^{2}}=4 ;[/math]
 আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta_{1}[/math]
 [math]=-\tan ^{-1} \frac{2}{2 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]=-\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
 [math]\therefore2\sqrt{3}-2\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]=4\mathrm{e}^{-i\frac{\pi}{6}}...........(i);[/math]
 [math]-2 \sqrt{3}+6 i[/math]
এর মডুলাস, [math]r_{2}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(-2 \sqrt{3})^{2}+(6)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=4 \sqrt{3}[/math]
আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta_{2}=\pi-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{6}{-2 \sqrt{3}}\right|[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \sqrt{3}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore-2 \sqrt{3}+6 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]=4 \sqrt{3} \mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i} \frac{2 \pi}{3}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore(2\sqrt{3}-2\mathrm{i})[/math]
 [math](-2\sqrt{3}+6\mathrm{i})[/math]
 [math]=4\mathrm{e}^{-i\frac{\pi}{6}}\times4\sqrt{3}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{2\pi}{3}}[/math]
 [math]=16\sqrt{3}e^{\left(-\frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{2\mathrm{\pi}}{3}\right)i}[/math]
 [math]=16\sqrt{3}e^{\mathrm{i}\frac{\pi}{2}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #167 (ID: 439507)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-\mathrm{i}}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt2[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt3[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]=\text {Let},\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-1}=\mathrm{x} \\\Rightarrow(\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-\mathrm{i}})^{2}=\mathrm{x}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(\sqrt{i})^2+2.\sqrt{i},\sqrt{-i}+(\sqrt{-i})^2=x^2\\\Rightarrow x^2=2\\\Rightarrow x=\pm\sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #168 (ID: 439508)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math]-1+\sqrt{3} i[/math] জটিল সংখ্যাটির মডুলাস কত?

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math]3[/math]
গ. [math]2[/math]
ঘ. [math]4[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{(-1)^{2}+(\sqrt{3})^{2}}=2[/math]

Question #169 (ID: 439509)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board, Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]|z-3|=2|z+3|[/math] দ্বারা নির্দেশিত সঞ্চারপথ কোনটি?

Options:
ক. বৃত্ত
খ. পরাবৃত্ত
গ. উপবৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]z\equiv x+i y \therefore \sqrt{(x-3)^{2}+y^{2}}=2 \sqrt{(x+3)^{2}+y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}+10 x+9=0[/math]

Explanation:

Question #170 (ID: 439510)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\sqrt[3]{x+i y}=p+i q[/math] হয় তবে-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{x}{p}+\frac{y}{q}=4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)[/math]
B. [math]\frac{x}{p}-\frac{y}{q}=4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)[/math]
C. [math]\frac{x}{p}+\frac{y}{q}=4\left(p^{2}+q^{2}\right)[/math]
D. [math]\frac{x}{p}-\frac{y}{q}=4\left(p^{2}+q^{2}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]x+i y=p^{3}+2 p^{2} i q[/math]
 [math]+3 p i^{2} q^{2}+i^{3} q^{3}[/math]
 [math]=\left(p^{3}-3 p q^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]+i\left(3 p^{2} q-q^{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore x[/math]
 [math]=p^{3}-3 p q^{2} ;[/math]
 [math]y=3 p^{2} q-q^{3}[/math]
 [math]\frac{p^{3}-3 p q^{2}}{p}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{3 p^{2} q-q}{q}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{p\left(p^{2}-3 q^{2}\right)}{p}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{q\left(3 p^{2}-q^{2}\right)}{q}[/math]
 [math]=p^{2}-3 q^{2}[/math]
 [math]+3 p^{2}-q^{2}[/math]
 [math]=4 p^{2}-4 q^{2}=[/math]
 [math]4\left(p^{2}-q^{2}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #171 (ID: 439511)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\omega[/math] যদি এককের জটিল ঘনমূল হয় তবে [math]\left(\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{21}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-2[/math]
খ. [math]-1[/math]
গ. [math]1[/math]
ঘ. [math]\omega[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\left(\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{21}[/math]
 [math]=(-1)^{21}=1[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0\right.[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(\omega+\omega^{2}=-1\right][/math]
 [math]=(-1)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #172 (ID: 439512)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]a+i b=0[/math] হলে [math]a^{2}+b^{2}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]-1[/math]
C. [math]i[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]|a+i b|=\sqrt{a^{2}+b^{2}}=0 \\\Rightarrow a^{2}+b^{2}=0[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]z=x+i y[/math] হয়, তবে [math]|z-5|=3[/math] বৃত্তের কেন্দ্র কত?

Options:
ক. [math](0,0)[/math]
খ. [math](3,5)[/math]
গ. [math](3,0)[/math]
ঘ. [math](5,0)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]|x+i y-5|=3 \\\Rightarrow|x-5+i y|=3 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x-5)^{2}+y^{2}}=9 \\\Rightarrow x^{2}-10 x+25+y^{2}=9[/math]
কেন্দ্র [math](5,0)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #175 (ID: 439515)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit), Pabna University of Science and Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]i^{2}=-1[/math] হলে [math]\frac{i^{-1}-i}{2 i^{-1}+i}[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]-2i[/math]
B. [math]2i[/math]
C. [math]-2[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i^{-1}-i}{2 i^{-1}+i}=\frac{\frac{1}{i}-i}{\frac{2}{i}+i}=\frac{\frac{1-i^{2}}{i}}{\frac{2+i^{2}}{i}}=\frac{1+1}{2-1}=2[/math]

Explanation:

Question #176 (ID: 439516)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-64[/math]
খ. [math]-4[/math]
গ. [math]-8[/math]
ঘ. [math]-16[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=\left(-2 \omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=-8 \omega^{6}[/math]
 [math]=-8[/math]


Explanation:

Question #177 (ID: 439517)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangabandhu Sheikh Mujibur Rahman Science and Technology University, Gopalganj (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{2 a}=1+i[/math] হলে [math]a^{6}+a^{4}+a^{2}+1[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]3[/math]
খ. [math]-1[/math]
গ. [math]0[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}} ; a^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(1+i)^{2}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2 i}{2}=1 \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore i^{3}+1^{2}+i+1[/math]
 [math]=-i-1+i+1[/math]
 [math]=0[/math]


Explanation:

Question #180 (ID: 439520)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]- 2 - 2i[/math] জটিল সংখ্যার আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{-2}{-2}\right|=\tan ^{-1}(1)=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
মূখ্য আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=\theta-\pi=\frac{\pi}{4}-\pi=\frac{-3 \pi}{4}[/math]
গৌন্য আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=\pi+\frac{\pi}{4}=\frac{5 \pi}{4}[/math]
অপশনে মূখ্য আর্গুমেন্ট ও গৌন্য আর্গুমেন্ট দুটোই দেওয়া থাকলে মূখ্য আর্গুমেন্ট দাগাতে হবে।

Explanation:

 [math]=\pm(1-3 i)[/math]

Question #186 (ID: 439526)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Chittagong University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]a=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math] এবং [math]\mathrm{b}=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})[/math] হয়,তবে [math]a^{4}+a^{2} b^{2}+b^{4}[/math] এর মান কোনটি হয়?

Options:
A. [math]-a+b+1=0[/math]
B. [math]a+b+1=0[/math]
C. [math]a-b+1=0[/math]
D. None


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]a=\frac{1}{2}[/math]
 [math](-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math]
 [math]=\omega[/math]
 এবং [math]b=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})=\omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]a^4+a^2b^2+b^4[/math]
 [math]=\omega^4+\omega^2.\[/math]
 [math]\omega^4+\omega^8[/math]
 [math]=\omega^3.\omega+[/math]
 [math]\left(\omega^3\right)^2[/math]
 [math]+(\omega^3)^2.\omega^2[/math]
 [math]=\omega+1+\omega^2[/math]
 [math]\left[\because\omega^3=1\right];[/math]
 [math]a+b+1=0[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 1+\omega + \omega^2 = 0 ]\right.[/math]


Explanation:

Question #188 (ID: 439528)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]|x+i y|=5[/math] কার সমীকরণ?

Options:
ক. সরলরেখা
খ. অধিবৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. বৃত্ত
ঙ. উপবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]|x+i y|=5 \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}=5 \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=5^{2},[/math]
 বৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Explanation:
