Question #189 (ID: 439529)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{-3+\sqrt{-3+\sqrt{-3+\ldots \ldots \ldots \infty}}}=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]-\sqrt{3} i[/math]
B. [math]\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{11} i}{2}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1 \pm \sqrt{-11}}{2}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{1 \pm \sqrt{11}}{2}[/math]
E. None


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{-3+\sqrt{-3+\ldots \ldots \ldots}}=x[/math]
 [math]\therefore-3+\sqrt{-3+\sqrt{-3+\ldots .}}=\mathrm{x}^{2}[/math]
বা, [math]x^{2}+3=x[/math]
বা, [math]x^{2}-x+3=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\frac{1 \pm \sqrt{1-12}}{2}=\frac{1 \pm \sqrt{-11}}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #190 (ID: 439530)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Rajshahi University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[3]{-i}[/math] এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]-i, \frac{1}{2}(i \pm \sqrt{3})[/math]
খ. [math]i, \frac{1}{2}(-i \pm \sqrt{3})[/math]
গ. [math]0, \frac{1}{2}(\mathrm{i} \pm \sqrt{3})[/math]
ঘ. [math]0, \frac{1}{2}(-i \pm \sqrt{3})[/math]
ঙ. None


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt[3]{-i}=\sqrt[3]{i^{3}}=x \quad \therefore x^{3}=i^{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=i, i \omega, i \omega^{2}=i, i\left(\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{3} i}{2}\right)=i,\left(\frac{-i \pm \sqrt{3}}{2}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]\text { The value of } \sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\cdots \infty}}} \text { is- }[/math]

Options:
A. [math]\frac{1\pm i}{2}[/math]
B. [math]\pm(1+i)[/math]
C. [math]\pm(1-i)[/math]
D. [math]\pm\left(\frac{1}{2}+i\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]x=\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\sqrt{-\frac{1}{2}+\cdots}}}\\\Rightarrow x^2=-\frac{1}{2}+x\Rightarrow x^2-x+\frac{1}{2}=0\\\Rightarrow x=\frac{1\pm i}{2}[/math]

Question #192 (ID: 439532)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math] এর মান এবং নতি হবে যথাক্রমে-

Options:
ক. [math]0.0[/math]
খ. [math]-2 \mathrm{i},-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{i}, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2, \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{i^{2}-1}{i^{2}+2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-1}{-1+2}=-2[/math]
 [math]-2=-2+\mathrm{i} 0, \theta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{0}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 0=\pi[/math]
N.B: বিপরীতিক ফাংশনের মান সর্বদা ধনাত্মক হবে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 0=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\pi \therefore \theta=[/math]
 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]\sqrt2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{i}=\sqrt{\frac{2i}{2}}=\sqrt{\frac{(1+i)^2}{2}}=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}};\sqrt{i}=\frac{1-i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i}=\frac{1+i+1-i}{\sqrt{2}}=\frac{2}{\sqrt{2}}=\sqrt{2}[/math]

Question:

Options:
A. [math]-2i[/math]
B. [math]2i[/math]
C. [math]-2[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i^{-1}-i}{2 i^{-1}+i}=\frac{\frac{1}{i}-i}{\frac{2}{i}+i}=\frac{\frac{1-i^{2}}{i}}{\frac{2+i^{2}}{i}}=\frac{1+1}{2-1}=2[/math]

Question #196 (ID: 439371)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)

Question:
যদি z = x + iy এবং |z-2| = 3 হয়, কেন্দ্র কোনটি?

Options:
ক. (3, 0)
খ. (0, 2)
গ. (2, 0)
ঘ. (0, 3)


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]|z-2|=3[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow|\mathrm{x}+\mathrm{iy}-2|=3 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{(\mathrm{x}-2)^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}}=3 \\\Rightarrow(\mathrm{x}-2)^{2}+(\mathrm{y}-0)^{2}=3^{2}\end{array}[/math]
এর কেন্দ্র = (2, 0) এবং ব্যাসার্ধ = 3

খ. [math]x^{2}+12=0[/math]

ঘ. [math]x^{2}+6=0[/math]

 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow|1-i x+1|=4 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{2^{2}+(-x)^{2}}=4 \\\Rightarrow 4+x^{2}=16\end{array}[/math]

ঘ. [math]\frac{5\pi}{3}[/math]

 কে আকারে [math]z = re^{i\theta}[/math]
 প্রকাশ করতে হলে, [math]\tan\theta = \frac{-2}{-2} = 1[/math]
 → [math]\theta = \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 কিন্তু তৃতীয় চতুর্ভুজে হওয়ায় [math]\theta = \pi + \frac{\pi}{4} = \frac{5\pi}{4}[/math]

Question:
[math]\mathbf{z}_{1}=3+2 \mathbf{i}[/math] এবং [math]z_{2}=3-2 \mathbf{i}[/math] হলে, [math]\mathbf{z}_{1}^{2}+\mathbf{z}_{1} \mathbf{z}_{2}+\mathbf{z}_{2}^{2}[/math] মান কত?

C. [math]23[/math]
D. [math]24[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(3+2 i+3-2 i)^{2}-(3+2 i)(3-2 i) \\=6^{2}-\left(3^{2}+2^{2}\right)=36-13=23\end{array}[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1+\sqrt{3} i}{\sqrt{3}+i}[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
A. 60°
B. [math]\tan ^{-1} 3[/math]
C. 30°
D. 45°


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1+\sqrt{3} i}{\sqrt{3}+i}[/math]
 এর আগুমেন্ট [math]=\arg (1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i})[/math]
 [math]-\arg (\sqrt{3}+\mathrm{i})[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}(\sqrt{3})[/math]
 [math]-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=60^{\circ}-30^{\circ}=30^{\circ}[/math]

Question #203 (ID: 439378)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math](1+a i)^{2}[/math] জটিল রাশিটির আর্গুমেন্ট [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math] হলে, a এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]1 \pm \sqrt{2}[/math]
খ. [math]-1 \pm \sqrt{2}[/math]
গ. $ 2 \pm \sqrt{2} $
ঘ. [math]-2 \pm \sqrt{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math](1+a i)^{2}=1+2 a i+a^{2} i^{2}=1-a^{2}+i 2 a[/math]
প্রশ্নমতে,[math]\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{2 a}{1-a^{2}}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{2 a}{1-a^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\tan \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{2 a}{1-a^{2}}=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 2 a[/math]
 [math]=1-a^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a^{2}+2 a-1=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(a+1)^{2}=2[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a+1=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a=-1 \pm \sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

 এর মডুলাস [math]=\left(\frac{|1+2 \sqrt{2} i|}{|-1+2 \sqrt{2} i|}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{\sqrt{(2 \sqrt{2})^{2}+1^{2}}}{\sqrt{(2 \sqrt{2})^{2}+1^{2}}}\right)^{3}=1^{3}=1[/math]

Question #205 (ID: 439380)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\mathrm{z}=8 \mathrm{e}^{\frac{\mathrm{i} \pi}{3}}[/math] হয়, তবে [math]\left|e^{\mathrm{iz}}\right|[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]e^{-\sqrt{3}}[/math]
B. [math]e^{-2 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
C. [math]e^{-3 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
D. [math]e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\left|\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{iz}}\right|=[/math]
 [math]\left|\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i} 8} \mathrm{e}^{\frac{\mathrm{i}}{3}}\right|[/math]
 [math]=\left|\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i} 8}\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{3}+\mathrm{i} \sin \frac{\pi}{3}\right)\right|[/math]
 [math]=\left|e^{i 8}\left(\frac{1}{2}+i \frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)\right|[/math]
 [math]=\left|e^{4 i+} i^{2} 4 \sqrt{3}\right|[/math]
 [math]=\left|e^{4 i} \cdot e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}\right|[/math]
 [math]=\left|e^{4 i}\right|\left|e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}\right|[/math]
 [math]=|\cos 4 \theta+i \sin 4 \theta|[/math]
 [math]e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]=\left|\cos ^{2} 4 \theta+\sin ^{2} 4 \theta\right|[/math]
 [math]e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]=1 \times e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}[/math]
 [math]=e^{-4 \sqrt{3}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #209 (ID: 439384)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)

 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

Question #210 (ID: 439385)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\omega[/math] যদি এককের একটি জটিল (কাল্পনিক) ঘনমূল হয়, তবে [math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math] [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]4[/math]
B. [math]6[/math]
C. [math]3[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \omega^{4}=\omega^{3} \omega=\omega\right][/math]
 [math]=\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(-2 \omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=4 \omega^{3}=4[/math]


Explanation:

A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]-2[/math]
C. [math]-1[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

 [math]+\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}\right)^{2023}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2023}+\left(\omega^{2}\right)^{2023}[/math]
 [math]=\left(\omega^{3}\right)^{674} \cdot \omega+[/math]
 [math]\left(\omega^{3}\right)^{1348}[/math]
 [math]\cdot \omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\omega+\omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=-1[/math]

Question #213 (ID: 439388)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\boldsymbol{\omega}[/math] এবং [math]\mathbf{x}[/math] [math]=\mathbf{p}+\mathbf{q}, \mathbf{y}[/math] [math]=\mathbf{p} \omega+\mathbf{q}[/math] [math]\omega^{2},\mathrm{z}[/math] [math]=\mathrm{p} \omega^{2}[/math] [math]+\mathrm{q} \omega[/math] হলে, [math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]pq[/math]
B. [math]4pq[/math]
C. [math]5pq[/math]
D. [math]6pq[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x^{2}+y^{2}+z^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(p+q)^{2}+[/math]
 [math]\left(p \omega+q \omega^{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]+\left(p \omega^{2}+q \omega\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=p^{2}+2 p q+q^{2}[/math]
 [math]+p^{2} \omega^{2}+2 p q[/math]
 [math]\omega^{3}+q^{2}[/math]
 [math]\omega^{4}+p^{2} \omega^{4}[/math]
 [math]+2 p q \omega^{3}+q^{2} \omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=p^{2}+2 p q+q^{2}+p^{2}[/math]
 [math]\omega^{2}+2 p q+q^{2}[/math]
 [math]\omega+p^{2}[/math]
 [math]\omega+2 p q+q^{2}[/math]
 [math]\omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]=6 p q+p^{2}[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]+q^{2}\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=6 p q+0+0[/math]
 [math]=6 p q[/math]


Explanation:

Question #214 (ID: 439389)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)

Question:

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math]\omega^{2}[/math] অথবা [math]\frac{1}{2}(-1-i \sqrt{3})[/math]
গ. [math]\omega[/math] অথবা [math]\frac{1}{2}(-1+i \sqrt{3})[/math]
ঘ. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\omega^{-3002} =\frac{1}{\omega^{3002}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\left(\omega^{3}\right)^{1000} \times \omega^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\omega^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\omega^{3}}{\omega^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\omega=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i})[/math]

Question #215 (ID: 439390)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x+i y=\sqrt{\frac{p+i q}{r+i s}}[/math] হলে, [math]\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{p^{2}-q^{2}}{r^{2}+s^{2}}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{p^{2}+q^{2}}{r^{2}-s^{2}}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\mathrm{p}^{2}+\mathrm{q}^{2}}{\mathrm{r}^{2}+\mathrm{s}^{2}}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{p^{2}-q^{2}}{r^{2}-s^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]x+i y=\sqrt{\frac{p+i q}{r+i s}}[/math]
 [math]\ldots . . . . . . .(i)[/math]
 [math]x + iy[/math]
 এর জটিল অনুবন্ধী [math]= x - iy[/math]
 [math]\therefore x-i y=\sqrt{\frac{p-i q}{r-i s}}[/math]
 [math]\ldots . . . . . . . \text { (ii) }[/math]
 [math]\text { (i) } x(i i)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(x+i y)(x-i y)[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\left(\frac{p+i q}{r+i s}\right)$$\left(\frac{p-i q}{r-i s}\right)}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-i^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{\frac{p^{2}-i^{2} q^{2}}{r^{2}-i^{2} s^{2}} \Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}=\sqrt{\frac{p^{2}+q^{2}}{r^{2}+s^{2}}}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{p^{2}+q^{2}}{r^{2}+s^{2}}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #216 (ID: 439391)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[3]{a+i b}=x+i y[/math] হলে, [math]\frac{\mathbf{b}}{\mathbf{y}}-\frac{\mathbf{a}}{\mathbf{x}}=[/math] কত?

Options:
A. [math]2\left(x^{2}-y^{2}\right)[/math]
B. [math]2\left(y^{2}-x^{2}\right)[/math]
C. [math]-2\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)[/math]
D. [math]2\left(x^{2}+y^{2}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt[3]{a+i b}=x+i y[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a+i b=x^{3}+3 x^{2} i y+3[/math]
 [math]x i^{2} y^{2}+i^{3} y^{3}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a+i b=x^{3}+3 i x^{2}[/math]
 [math]y-3 x y^{2}-i y^{3}[/math]
বাস্তব ও অবাস্তব মান সমীকৃত করে পাই, [math]a=x^{3}-3 x y^{2},[/math]
 [math]b=3 x^{2} y-y^{3}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{\mathrm{b}}{\mathrm{y}}-\frac{\mathrm{a}}{\mathrm{x}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{3 \mathrm{x}^{2} \mathrm{y}-\mathrm{y}^{3}}{\mathrm{y}}[/math]
 [math]-\frac{\mathrm{x}^{3}-3 \mathrm{xy} \mathrm{y}^{2}}{\mathrm{x}}[/math]
 [math]=\left(3 \mathrm{x}^{2}-\mathrm{y}^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]-\left(\mathrm{x}^{2}-3 \mathrm{y}^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=2 \mathrm{x}^{2}+2\mathrm{y}^{2}[/math]
 [math]=2\left(\mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}\right)[/math]


Explanation:

 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=\tan ^{-1} \frac{3 / 2}{3 / 2}=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

 [math]\begin{array}{l}=i-1-i \\=-1\end{array}[/math]

Question:
[math]\mathrm{i}^{2}=-1[/math] হলে, [math]\frac{i^{-1}-i}{2i^{-1}+i}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]-2i[/math]
B. [math]2i[/math]
C. [math]-2[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i^{-1}-i}{2i^{-1}+i}=\frac{\frac{1}{1}-i}{2\frac{1}{i}+i}=\frac{1-i^2}{2+i^2}=\frac{1+1}{2-1}=2[/math]

Question #229 (ID: 439404)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math]1+\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}+\cdots+\omega^{16}[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]0[/math]
খ. [math]1[/math]
গ. [math]-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]\omega^2[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]1+\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}+\cdots+\omega^{16}=\omega^{0}+\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}+\cdots+\omega^{16}[/math]
ঘাতগুলো সমান্তর ধারা গঠন করে। তাহলে [math]16=0+(n-1) \times 2 \therefore n=9[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 মোট পদ [math]9[/math]
 টি। প্রতি পদের যোগফল ' [math]0[/math]
'। [math]\therefore[/math]
 ধারার যোগফল শুন্য।

Question #230 (ID: 439405)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Agriculture Cluster Admission System
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\frac{2+3i}{2-i}=A+iB[/math] এবং [math]A[/math] ও [math]B[/math] বাস্তব সংখ্যা হয় তাহলে [math]B[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{7}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{2+3i}{2-i}=A+iB\ \\\Rightarrow\frac{(2+3i)(2+i)}{(2-i)(2+i)}=A+iB[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\frac{4+6i+2i+3i^2}{2^2-i^2}=A+iB\\\Rightarrow\frac{4+8i-3}{4+1}=A+i B \\\Rightarrow \frac{1}{5}+\frac{8}{5} i=A+i B \therefore B=\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #231 (ID: 439406)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Khulna University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]z=x+iy[/math] হয়,তবে [math]|2 z-1|=|z-2|[/math] দ্বারা নির্দেশিত সঞ্চারপথের সমীকরণ হবে কোনটি?

Options:
ক. অধিবৃত্ত
খ. উপবৃত্ত
গ. এককবৃত্ত
ঘ. পরাবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]z=x+iy\ ;|2z-1|=|z-2|\ \\\Rightarrow\ |2(x+iy)-1|=|x+iy-2|[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(2 x-1)^{2}+(2 y)^{2}}=\sqrt{(x-2)^{2}+y^{2}} \\\Rightarrow 4 x^{2}+1-4 x+4 y^{2}=x^{2}+4+y^{2}-4 x[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow 3 x^{2}+3 y^{2}=3 \therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1 \therefore[/math]
 সমীকরণটি একক বৃত্তের,কারণ ব্যাসার্ধ [math]1[/math]
 একক।অথবা,কোন জটিল সংখ্যা [math]z=x+iy[/math]
 এর জন্য [math]\left|a z+k_{1}\right|=\left|b z+k_{2}\right|[/math]
 সর্বদা বৃত্ত নির্দেশ করে।

Explanation:

Question #232 (ID: 439407)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: Agriculture Cluster Admission System
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\frac{2+3 i}{2-i}=A+i B[/math] এবং [math]A[/math] ও [math]B[/math] বাস্তব সংখ্যা হয় তাহলে [math]B[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{7}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{2+3 i}{2-i}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(2+3 i)(2+i)}{(2-i)(2+i)}[/math]
 [math]=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{4+6 i+2 i+3 i^{2}}{2^{2}-i^{2}}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{4+8 i-3}{4+1}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{5}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{8}{5} i=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\therefore B=\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #234 (ID: 439409)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math] এর মান এবং নতি হবে যথাক্রমে-

Options:
ক. [math]0.0[/math]
খ. [math]-2 \mathrm{i},-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{i}, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2, \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{i^{2}-1}{i^{2}+2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-1}{-1+2}=-2[/math]
 [math]-2=-2+\mathrm{i} 0, \theta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{0}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 0=\pi[/math]
N.B: বিপরীতিক ফাংশনের মান সর্বদা ধনাত্মক হবে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 0=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\pi \therefore \theta=[/math]
 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

Explanation:

C. [math]23[/math]
D. [math]24[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

 [math]\begin{array}{l}=(3+2 i+3-2 i)^{2}-(3+2 i)(3-2 i) \\=6^{2}-\left(3^{2}+2^{2}\right)=36-13=23\end{array}[/math]

Question:
যদি [math]z=x+iy[/math] হয়,তবে [math]|z+i|=|\bar{z}+2|[/math] দ্বারা নির্দেশিত সঞ্চার পথের নাম কি হবে?

Options:
ক. সরলরেখা
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. উপবৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]|z+1|=|\bar{z}+2| \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{x^{2}+(y+1)^{2}}=\sqrt{(x+2)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}+y^{2}+2 y+1=x^{2}+4 x+4+y^{2} \\\Rightarrow 4 x-2 y+3=0[/math]
যা সরলরেখার সঞ্চারপথ নির্দেশ করে।

 [math]=16[/math]

Question:
[math]\frac{1}{1-i}[/math] এর বাস্তব ও কাল্পনিক অংশের সমষ্টি কত?

খ. [math]1[/math]
গ. [math]2[/math]
ঘ. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{1-i}=\frac{1+i}{(1-i)(1+i)}=\frac{1+i}{1^{2}+1^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2} i \quad \therefore[/math]
 সমষ্টি [math]=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}=1[/math]

 [math]\therefore x= \pm i, \pm 1[/math]

[math]1-\frac{1}{1\frac{1}{1+i}}[/math] এর মডুলাস ও আর্গুমেন্ট কোনটি?

ক. [math]1,0[/math]
খ. [math]1, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]1, \pi[/math]
ঘ. [math]1, \frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:

 [math]=1-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{1+i}}[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{1+i}{1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1}{1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-i}{i^2}[/math]
 [math]=i[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 মডুলাস [math]=\sqrt{0^{2}+(1)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=1[/math]
 এবং আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{0}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে, [math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{3}-\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]4[/math]
C. [math]-4[/math]
D. [math]0[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]-\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]-\left(-\omega^{2}-\omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]-(-\omega-\omega)^{3} \quad[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0\right][/math]
 [math]=\left(-2 \omega^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]-(-2 \omega)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=-8 \omega^{6}+8 \omega^{3}[/math]
 [math]=-8+8[/math]
 [math]=0[/math]

Question #253 (ID: 439428)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018 | Boards:  | Tags: Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\mathrm{x}=2-\mathrm{i}[/math] হয়,তবে [math]x^{3}-3 x^{2}+x+10[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]5[/math]
B. [math]-5[/math]
C. [math]\pm5[/math]
D. সবগুলোই


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]x=2-i\\\Rightarrow x-2=-i\\\Rightarrow x^2+4-4x=-1\\\Rightarrow x^2-4x+5=0[/math]
এখন [math]x^{3}-3 x^{2}+x+10=x[/math]
 [math]\left(x^{2}-4 x+5\right)[/math]
 [math]+\left(x^{2}-4 x+5\right)+[/math]
 [math]5[/math]
 [math]=x \times 0+0+5[/math]
 [math]=5[/math]


Explanation:

Question #254 (ID: 439429)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017-2018, 2012-2013 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit), Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-\mathrm{i}}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]2[/math]
B. [math]\sqrt2[/math]
C. [math]\sqrt3[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]=\text {Let},\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-1}=\mathrm{x} \\\Rightarrow(\sqrt{\mathrm{i}}+\sqrt{-\mathrm{i}})^{2}=\mathrm{x}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(\sqrt{i})^2+2.\sqrt{i},\sqrt{-i}+(\sqrt{-i})^2=x^2\\\Rightarrow x^2=2\\\Rightarrow x=\pm\sqrt{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]-1+\sqrt{3} i[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{2 \pi}{3}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{Z}=-1+\sqrt{3}\mathrm{i}\ ;\ \arg\mathrm{Z}=\pi-\tan^{-1}\left(\frac{\sqrt{3}}{1}\right)=\frac{2}{3}\pi[/math]

Question:
[math]-625[/math] এর চতুর্থ মূল কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]
খ. [math]\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}(21 \pm i)[/math]
গ. [math]\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}(5 \pm i)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\pm \frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}(1 \pm i)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x^4=-625[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^2=\pm25i[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=\pm\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\left[\sqrt{25+0}\pm i\sqrt{25-0}\right][/math]
 [math]=\pm\frac{5}{\sqrt{2}}(1\pm i)[/math]


Explanation:

Question #259 (ID: 439434)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math] এর মান এবং নতি হবে যথাক্রমে-

Options:
ক. [math]0.0[/math]
খ. [math]-2 \mathrm{i},-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{i}, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2, \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{i^{2}-1}{i^{2}+2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-1}{-1+2}=-2[/math]
 [math]-2=-2+\mathrm{i} 0, \theta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{0}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 0=\pi[/math]
N.B: বিপরীতিক ফাংশনের মান সর্বদা ধনাত্মক হবে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 0=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\pi \therefore \theta=[/math]
 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #264 (ID: 439439)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\left(\begin{array}{cc}1+2 i & 0 \\0 & 1-2 i\end{array}\right)\left(\begin{array}{l}x \\y\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}{l}0 \\1\end{array}\right)[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] ও [math]y[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]x=y=0[/math]
B. [math]x\ne0,\frac{1}{5}(1+i2)[/math]
C. [math]x\ne0,\frac{1}{5}(1-i2)[/math]
D. [math]x=0,y=\frac{1+i2}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\left[\begin{array}{cc}1+2 i & 0 \\0 & 1-2 i\end{array}\right]^{-1}=\frac{1}{5}[/math]
 [math]\left[\begin{array}{cc}1-2 i & 0 \\0 & 1+2 i\end{array}\right][/math]
 [math]\left[\begin{array}{l}x \\5\end{array}\right]=\frac{1}{5}[/math]
 [math]\left[\begin{array}{cc}1-2 i & 0 \\0 & 1+2 i\end{array}\right][/math]
 [math]\left[\begin{array}{l}0 \\1\end{array}\right][/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{5}\left[\begin{array}{c}0\\1+2i\end{array}\right][/math]
 [math]=\left[\begin{array}{c}0\\\frac{(1+2i)}{5}\end{array}\right][/math]
 [math]\therefore x=0,\ y=\frac{1+2i}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math]z_{1}=a+i b[/math] হলে [math]z[/math] এর কোন মানের জন্য [math]z_{1} z=1[/math] হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{a^2+b^2}(a+ib)[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{1}{\left.|a+ib\right|^{2}}(a-ib)[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{1}{|a+ib \mid}(a-ib)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{a}{a^{2}+b^{2}} \pm \frac{b}{a^{2}+b^{2}}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]Z_{1} Z=1 ; Z=\frac{1}{z_{1}}=\frac{1}{a+ib}=\frac{a-ib}{a^{2}+b^{2}}=\frac{1}{\left.|a+ib\right|^{2}}(a-ib)[/math]

Explanation:

Question #268 (ID: 439443)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Shahjalal University of Science & Technology (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]a^{3}+b^{3}+c^{3}-3 a b c[/math] এর সঠিক উৎপাদক বিশ্লেষণ কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](a+b+c)\left(a+b \omega+c \omega^{2}\right)\left(a+b \omega^{2}+c \omega\right)[/math]
খ. [math](a+b+c)\left(a-b \omega+c \omega^{2}\right)\left(a+b \omega^{2}+c \omega\right)[/math]
গ. [math](a+b+c)\left(a+b \omega+c \omega^{2}\right)\left(a+b \omega^{2}-c \omega\right)[/math]
ঘ. [math](a+b+c)\left(a+b-c \omega^{2}\right)\left(a+b \omega^{2}+c \omega\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\text{Hints:}\\\text{- } a^{3}+b^{3}+c^{3}-3 a b c[/math]
 [math]=(a+b+c)[/math]
 [math]\left(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2}-a b-b c-c a\right)[/math]
 [math]\text{- } 1+\omega[/math]
 [math]+\omega^{2}=0[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow-1[/math]
 [math]=\omega+\omega^{2}\\\text{- } \omega^{3}=1[/math]
 [math]\text{Solve:}\\\text { Option A }\\(a+b+c)\left(a+b \omega+c \omega^{2}\right)\left(a+b \omega^{2}+c \omega\right)=(a+b+c)[/math]
 [math]\left(a^{2}+a b \omega^{2}+a c \omega+a b \omega+b^{2} \omega^{2}+b c \omega+a c \omega^{2}+b c \omega^{4}+c^{2} \omega^{3}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(a+b+c)[/math]
 [math]\left[a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2}+\left(\omega+\omega^{2}\right) a b+\left(\omega+\omega^{2}\right) b c+\left(\omega+\omega^{2}\right) a c\right][/math]
 [math]=(a+b+c)[/math]
 [math]\left(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2}-a b-b c-c a\right) \quad[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0\right] \\=a^{3}+b^{3}+c^{3}-3 a b c[/math]


Explanation:
ব্যাখ্যা: কোন রাশির প্রকৃত উৎপাদকগুলোকে গুণ করলে ঐ রাশি পাওয়া যায় । তাই option এর রাশিগুলি গুণ করে ঐ রাশি পাওয়া গেলে সেটিই সঠিক উত্তর।

Question #271 (ID: 439446)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore University of Science & Technology (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]x=-1+i[/math] হলে, [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}+4 x+7[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]6+i[/math]
খ. [math]5[/math]
গ. [math]9+i[/math]
ঘ. [math]8[/math]
ঙ. [math]1+i[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]x+1=i \\\Rightarrow x^{2}+2 x+1=i^{2}=-1 \\\Rightarrow x^{2}+2 x+2=0[/math]
এখন, [math]x^{3}+3 x^{2}[/math]
 [math]+4 x+7[/math]
 [math]=x\left(x^{2}+2 x+2\right)+x^{2}[/math]
 [math]+2 x+2+5=5[/math]


Explanation:

Question #272 (ID: 439447)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2016-2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Sylhet Agricultural University
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]i=\sqrt{-1}[/math] হলে [math]2i[/math] এর বর্গমূল কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\pm(1+i)[/math]
খ. [math]\pm(1-i)[/math]
গ. [math]1 \pm i[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2 i[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{2 \mathrm{i}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{1+2 \mathrm{i}-1}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{1+2 \mathrm{i}+\mathrm{i}^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\left[\therefore \mathrm{i}=\sqrt{-1} \therefore \mathrm{i}^{2}=-1\right][/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{i})^{2}}=[/math]
 [math]\pm(1+\mathrm{i})[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
[math](-1+\sqrt{-3})^{3}+(-1-\sqrt{-3})^{3}[/math] এর মান কোনটি ?

Options:
ক. [math]-16[/math]
খ. [math]12[/math]
গ. [math]8[/math]
ঘ. [math]16[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](-1-\sqrt{-3})^{3}+(-1-\sqrt{-3})^{3}[/math]
 [math]=\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)^{3} \cdot 2^{3}+\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]\cdot 2^{3}[/math]
 [math]=(\omega)^{3} \cdot 2^{3}+(\omega)^{3} \cdot 2^{3}[/math]
 [math]=1 \cdot 8+1 \cdot 8=16[/math]

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কোন দ্বিঘাত সমীকরণের একটি মূল [math]2-3i[/math] হলে সমীকরণটি হয়-

Options:
ক. [math]x^{2}+4 x+13=0[/math]
খ. [math]x^{2}-4 x-13=0[/math]
গ. [math]x^{2}-4 x+17=0[/math]
ঘ. [math]x^{2}-4 x+13=0[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x=2-3 i[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x-2=-3 i[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-4 x+4=-9[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}-4 x+13=0[/math]

Question #276 (ID: 439451)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে, [math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{2}+\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{2}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]-4[/math]
B. [math]-8[/math]
C. [math]16[/math]
D. [math]4[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)^{2}+[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega-\omega^{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=(-2 \omega)^{2}+[/math]
 [math]\left(-2 \omega^{2}\right)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=4 \omega^{2}+4 \omega^{4}[/math]
 [math]=4\left(\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]=4\left(\omega^{2}+\omega\right)=[/math]
 [math]4(-1)=-4[/math]


Explanation:

 [math]\pm2i\left(\frac{-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\pm2i,\pm(-i-\sqrt{3}),\pm(-i+\sqrt{3})=[/math]
 [math]\pm2i,\pm(i\pm\sqrt{3})[/math]

Question:
[math]x=-1+i \sqrt{2}[/math] হলে [math]x^{4}+4 x^{3}+6 x^{2}+4 x=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]3[/math]
B. [math]5[/math]
C. [math]7[/math]
D. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
এখন, [math]x^{4}+4 x^{3}[/math]
 [math]+6 x^{2}+4 x[/math]
 [math]=x^{2}\left(x^{2}+2 x+3\right)[/math]
 [math]+2 x\left(x^{2}+2 x+3\right)[/math]
 [math]-\left(x^{2}+2 x+3\right)+3[/math]
 [math]=x^2\times0+2x\times0-0+3=3;\[/math]
 [math]x=-1+i\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\ x+1=i\sqrt{2}\\\Rightarrow\ x^2+2x+1=-2\\\Rightarrow\ x^2+2x+3=0[/math]

Question #282 (ID: 439325)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math] এর মান এবং নতি হবে যথাক্রমে-

Options:
ক. [math]0.0[/math]
খ. [math]-2 \mathrm{i},-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{i}, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2, \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{i^{2}-1}{i^{2}+2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-1}{-1+2}=-2[/math]
 [math]-2=-2+\mathrm{i} 0, \theta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{0}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 0=\pi[/math]
N.B: বিপরীতিক ফাংশনের মান সর্বদা ধনাত্মক হবে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 0=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\pi \therefore \theta=[/math]
 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

Correct Answer: ঘ

আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}}{0}\right)=-\tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{2}=-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question #287 (ID: 439330)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\omega[/math] যদি এককের একটি জটিল (কাল্পনিক) ঘনমূল হয়, তবে [math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math] [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]4[/math]
B. [math]6[/math]
C. [math]3[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \omega^{4}=\omega^{3} \omega=\omega\right][/math]
 [math]=\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(-2 \omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=4 \omega^{3}=4[/math]


Explanation:

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 1996-1997, 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\frac{2-i}{2+i}=A+i B[/math] হলে, A এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-\frac{4}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]A+i B=\frac{2-i}{2+i}=\frac{(2-i)^{2}}{2^{2}-i^{2}}=\frac{4-4 i+i^{2}}{4+1}=\frac{4-4 i-1}{5}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{3}{5}-\frac{4}{5} \mathrm{i} \therefore \mathrm{A}=\frac{3}{5}[/math]

Question:
যদি [math]x=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math] এবং [math]y=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})[/math] হয় তবে [math](1-x-y+x y)[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]2[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x=\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})[/math]
 [math]=\omega ;[/math]
 [math]y=\frac{1}{2}(-1-\sqrt{-3})[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2}[/math]
 [math]1-x-y+xy[/math]
 [math]=1-\omega-\omega^2+\omega.\omega^2[/math]
 [math]=1-\omega-\omega^2+\omega^3[/math]
 [math]=1-\left(\omega+\omega^2\right)[/math]
 [math]+1\left[\omega^3=1\right][/math]
 [math]=1-(-1)+1[/math]
 [math]\left[\because 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0\right][/math]
 [math]=1+1+1=3[/math]

Question #293 (ID: 439336)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]a=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math] হয় তবে [math]a^6[/math] এর মান হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]-1[/math]
খ. [math]i[/math]
গ. [math]1[/math]
ঘ. [math]-i[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]a=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a^{2}=\frac{1+i^{2}+2 i}{2} \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore a^{2}=i ; a^{6}[/math]
 [math]=\left(a^{2}\right)^{3}[/math]
 [math]=i^{3}=-i[/math]
বিকল্পঃ [math]a=\frac{1+i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}+\frac{i}{\sqrt{2}}[/math]
 [math]=e^i{\left(\frac{\pi}{4}\right)}\quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore a^6[/math]
 [math]=\left(e^{i\left(\frac{\pi}{4}\right)}\right)^6[/math]
 [math]=e^i{\left(\frac{3\pi}{2}\right)}[/math]
 [math]=\cos\frac{3\pi}{3}+i\sin\frac{3\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]=-i[/math]


Explanation:

Question #294 (ID: 439337)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2010-2011 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Engineering & Technology
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]n[/math] এর ধনাত্বক সর্বনিম্ন অখন্ড মান বের কর যার জন্যে [math]\left(\frac{1+i}{1-i}\right)^{n}=1[/math] হয়।

Options:
ক. [math]2[/math]
খ. [math]3[/math]
গ. [math]6[/math]
ঘ. [math]4[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\left(\frac{1+i}{1-i}\right)^n=1\\\Rightarrow\left\{\frac{(1+i)^2}{(1-i)(1+i)}\right\}^n=1\ \\\Rightarrow\ i^n=1[/math]
এক্ষেত্রে ক্ষুদ্রতম [math]n=4[/math]
 এর জন্য [math]i^{4}=1 \quad \therefore n=4[/math]


Explanation:

Question #296 (ID: 439339)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021 | Boards:  | Tags: Engg Guccho (Ka Unit – Without Architecture)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\alpha, \beta \in \mathbb{R}, i^{2}=-1[/math] এবং [math]\frac{1-i x}{1+i x}=\alpha-i \beta[/math] হলে [math]x[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\alpha}{(1+\beta)}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{1}{(1+a)}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{1}{(1+\beta)}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{\beta}{(1+\alpha)}[/math]
E. None of them


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1-i x}{1+i x}[/math]
 [math]\times \frac{1-i x}{1-i x}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-x^{2}-21 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-x^{2}}{1+x^{2}}-i \frac{2 x}{1+x^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\alpha-i \beta[/math]
 [math]\beta=\frac{2x}{1+x^2};\alpha[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-x^2}{1+x^2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a+1[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-x^2+1+x^2}{1+x^2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow a+1=\frac{2}{1+x^2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{a+1}{\beta}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\frac{2}{1+x^{2}}}{\frac{1}{1+x^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2}{2 x}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{x}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x=\frac{\beta}{\alpha+1}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #297 (ID: 439340)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Question:
[math](1, -\sqrt{3})[/math] বিন্দুটির পোলার স্থানাঙ্ক

Options:
ক. [math](5, -\pi/4)[/math]
খ. [math](-2, \pi/4)[/math]
গ. [math](2, \frac{5\pi}{3})[/math]
ঘ. [math](2, \frac{\pi}{3})[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]r = \sqrt{1^2 + (-\sqrt{3})^2} = 2[/math]
, [math]\theta = \tan^{-1}(-\sqrt{3}) = -\pi/3[/math]
, তাই ধনাত্মক কোণে রূপান্তর করলে [math]\frac{5\pi}{3}[/math]

Question:
[math]i=\sqrt{-1}[/math] হলে [math]1+i+i^{2}+\ldots \ldots \ldots+i^{39}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. 1
B. -1
C. 0
D. i


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
সমষ্টি [math]=1+\frac{i\left(1-i^{39}\right)}{1-i}=1+\frac{i(1+i)}{1-i}=1-1=0[/math]

Question #301 (ID: 439344)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2020-2021 | Boards:  | Tags: General and Science Technology (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{p+4 i}=q+i[/math] হলে [math]p-q[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. [math]0[/math]
B. [math]1[/math]
C. [math]3[/math]
D. [math]5[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\sqrt{p+4 i}[/math]
 [math]=q+i[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow p+4 i[/math]
 [math]=(q+i)^{2}[/math]
 [math]=q^{2}+i^{2}+2 q i[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow p+4 i[/math]
 [math]=q^{2}-1+i 2 q[/math]
এখন, [math]2 q=4[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow q=2 ;[/math]
 [math]p=q^{2}-1[/math]
 [math]=2^{2}-1=3[/math]
 [math]\therefore p-q=3-2=1[/math]


Explanation:

Question #302 (ID: 439345)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2024-2025 | Boards:  | Tags: Bangladesh University of Textiles
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math]\omega^{12} + \omega^{14} + \omega^{16}[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. 0
খ. 1
গ. [math]\omega^2[/math]
ঘ. -1


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
যেহেতু [math]\omega^3 = 1[/math]
, অতএব [math]\omega^{12} = 1[/math]
, [math]\omega^{14} = \omega^2[/math]
, [math]\omega^{16} = \omega^1[/math]
। এগুলোর যোগফল [math]1 + \omega + \omega^2 = 0[/math]
।

Question #305 (ID: 439348)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2024-2025 | Boards:  | Tags: Hajee Mohammad Danesh Science & Technology University (B Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt{p} + 4i = q + i[/math] হলে [math]p - q[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
A. 1
B. 2
C. 3
D. 4


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
সমীকরণটি থেকে বাস্তব অংশ তুলনা করে পাই [math]\sqrt{p} = q \Rightarrow p = q^2[/math]
। তাই [math]p - q = q^2 - q[/math]
। যদি [math]q = 2[/math]
 হয়, তবে [math]p = 4[/math]
 এবং [math]p - q = 2[/math]
।

Explanation:

Question #306 (ID: 439349)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{Arg}(z)=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math] হলে [math]\operatorname{Arg}\left(i^{2} z\right)=[/math] কোনটি ?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{3}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{Arg}(z)=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow \operatorname{Arg}(x+i y)=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow \tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 [math]\operatorname{Arg}\left(i^{2} z\right)=\operatorname{Arg}\left(i^{2}(x+i y)\right)=\operatorname{Arg}\left(i^{2} x+i^{3} y\right)[/math]
 [math]=\operatorname{Arg}(-x-i y)=\pi+\tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}=\pi+\frac{\pi}{3}=\frac{4 \pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #307 (ID: 439350)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
(1+i) (x + iy) = 2 + 4i হলে, '5x' এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. 11
খ. 13
গ. 14
ঘ. 15


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](1+i)(x+i y)=2+4 i[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x+i y+i x+i^{2} y=2+4 i \Rightarrow x-y+i(x+y)=2+4 i[/math]
বাস্তব ও কাল্পনিক অংশ সমীকৃত , [math]x-y=2 ... (i)[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}x+y=4 \ldots \ldots \ldots . \text { (ii) } \\\text { (i) }+ \text { (ii) } \Rightarrow 2 x=6 \Rightarrow x=3 \Rightarrow 5 x=15\end{array}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #308 (ID: 439351)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{1}{1+i}[/math] কে A+iB আকারে প্রকাশ করলে B এর মান কত হবে?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{1}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]-\frac{1}{2}[/math]
গ. 1
ঘ. -1


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]A+i B=\frac{1}{1+i}\\=\frac{1-i}{(1+i)(1-i)}\\=\frac{1-i}{1^{2}-i^{2}}\\=\frac{1-i}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}-\frac{i}{2} \quad \\\therefore B=-\frac{1}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #309 (ID: 439352)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=\frac{1-\mathbf{i}}{1+i}[/math] হলে Re(z) = কোনটি?

Options:
A. 0
B. -i
C. -1
D. 1


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\begin{aligned}z= & \frac{1-i}{1+i}=\frac{(1-i)(1-i)}{(1+i)(1-i)}=\frac{1-2 i+i^{2}}{1^{2}-i^{2}}=\frac{-2 i}{2}=-i \\& \therefore \operatorname{Re}(z)=0\end{aligned}[/math]

Explanation:

Question #312 (ID: 439359)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Chittagong (Unit D/D1)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
বাস্তব সংখ্যার উপসেট [math]S = \{x : 5 x ^ 2 - 16x + 3 \leq 0\}[/math] এর লঘিষ্ট ঊর্ধ্বসীমা কোনটি?

Options:
ক. -5
খ. -3
গ. 3
ঘ. 5


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]5 x^{2}-16 x+3 \leq 0[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow 5 x^{2}-15 x-x+3 \leq 0 \Rightarrow 5 x(x-3)-(x-3) \leq 0 \\\Rightarrow(x-3)(5 x-1) \leq 0 \Rightarrow(x-3)\left(x-\frac{1}{5}\right) \leq 0 \Rightarrow \frac{1}{5} \leq x \leq 3\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 লঘিষ্ট উর্ধ্বসীমা (supS) = 3 এবং গরিষ্ঠ নিম্নসীমা (infS) [math]\frac{1}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #313 (ID: 439360)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023, 2022-2023, 2021-2022, 2018-2019, 2016-2017, 2011-2012 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Islamic University, Bangladesh (A Unit), Jagannath University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math] এর মান এবং নতি হবে যথাক্রমে-

Options:
ক. [math]0.0[/math]
খ. [math]-2 \mathrm{i},-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{i}, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-2, \pi[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{i-i^{-1}}{i+2 i^{-1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{i^{2}-1}{i^{2}+2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-1}{-1+2}=-2[/math]
 [math]-2=-2+\mathrm{i} 0, \theta[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{0}{-2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1} 0=\pi[/math]
N.B: বিপরীতিক ফাংশনের মান সর্বদা ধনাত্মক হবে, [math]\tan ^{-1} 0=0[/math]
অথবা, [math]\pi \therefore \theta=[/math]
 হবে [math]\pi](D)[/math]
Shortcut:[math]\because[/math]
 মান [math]= – 2,[/math]
 শুধু অপশন D তে আছে। তাই উত্তর D হবে।

Explanation:

Question #314 (ID: 439361)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2014-2015, 2011-2012, 2007-2008, 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Chittagong (Unit A)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{(i+1)^{2}}{(i-1)^{4}}[/math] জটিল সংখ্যাটির আর্গুমেন্ট হবে-

Options:
ক. [math]\pi[/math]
খ. [math]-\pi[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{(i+1)^{2}}{(i-1)^{4}}=\frac{i^{2}+2 i+1}{\left(i^{2}-2 i+1\right)^{2}}=\frac{2 i}{4 i^{2}}=\frac{1}{2 i}=-\frac{i}{2}[/math]
আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=-\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{\frac{1}{2}}{0}\right)=-\tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{2}=-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #317 (ID: 439364)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2002-2003, 2022-2023, 2019-2020 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit), Jatiya Kabi Kazi Nazrul Islam University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\omega[/math] যদি এককের একটি জটিল (কাল্পনিক) ঘনমূল হয়, তবে [math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math] [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]4[/math]
B. [math]6[/math]
C. [math]3[/math]
D. [math]2[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left(1-\omega+\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}+\omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left[\because \omega^{4}=\omega^{3} \omega=\omega\right][/math]
 [math]=\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2 \omega\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(-2 \omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(-2\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=4 \omega^{3}=4[/math]


Explanation:

গ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Question #319 (ID: 439366)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 1996-1997, 2022-2023 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), Jahangirnagar University (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\frac{2-i}{2+i}=A+i B[/math] হলে, A এর মান কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]1[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]-\frac{4}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]A+i B=\frac{2-i}{2+i}=\frac{(2-i)^{2}}{2^{2}-i^{2}}=\frac{4-4 i+i^{2}}{4+1}=\frac{4-4 i-1}{5}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{3}{5}-\frac{4}{5} \mathrm{i} \therefore \mathrm{A}=\frac{3}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #320 (ID: 439367)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\operatorname{Arg}(x)=\frac{\pi}{3}[/math] হলে, Arg (iz) = কোনটি?

Options:
A. [math]\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
D. [math]\frac{4 \pi}{3}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\operatorname{Arg}(z)=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow \operatorname{Arg}(x+i y)=\frac{\pi}{3} \Rightarrow \frac{\pi}{3}=\tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow \frac{Y}{x}=\tan \frac{\pi}{3}=\sqrt{3} \Rightarrow y=\sqrt{3} x \\\operatorname{Arg}(i z)=\operatorname{Arg}(i(x+iy))=\operatorname{Arg}(i x-y)\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\theta=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \frac{x}{y}=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}=\pi-\frac{\pi}{6}=\frac{5 \pi}{6}[/math]


Explanation:

Question:
n ∈ N এবং এ এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল হলে [math]\omega^{n}+\omega^{n+1}+\omega^{n+2}=[/math] কোনটি

Options:
ক. 1
খ. -i
গ. i
ঘ. 0


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\omega^{\mathrm{n}}+\omega^{\mathrm{n}+1}+\omega^{\mathrm{n}+2}=\omega^{\mathrm{n}}\left(1+\omega+\omega^{2}\right)=\omega^{\mathrm{n}} \times 0=0[/math]

ক. 3
খ. 4
গ. 2
ঘ. 5

Question #327 (ID: 439274)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
যদি [math]\frac{2+3 i}{2-i}=A+i B[/math] এবং [math]A[/math] ও [math]B[/math] বাস্তব সংখ্যা হয়, তবে [math]B =[/math] কত?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{-8}{5}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{1}{5}[/math]
গ. [math]-\frac{1}{5}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\frac{2+3 i}{2-i}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{(2+3 i)(2+i)}{(2-i)(2+i)}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{4+6 i+2 i+3 i^{2}}{2^{2}-i^{2}}=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{4+8 i-3}{4+1}[/math]
 [math]=A+i B \Rightarrow \frac{1}{5}+\frac{8}{5} i=A+i B[/math]
 [math]\therefore B=\frac{8}{5}[/math]


Explanation:


Sub-Questions:

Question #328 (ID: 439275)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Barisal Board | Tags: Barisal Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\frac{2-i}{2+i}=A+i B[/math] হলে [math]A=?[/math]

Options:
A. [math]1[/math]
B. [math]\frac{4}{5}[/math]
C. [math]\frac{3}{5}[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{4}{5}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\frac{2-i}{2+i}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{2-i}{2+i} \times \frac{2-i}{2-i}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{(2-i)^{2}}{2^{2}-(i)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{4-4 i+i^{2}}{4-(-1)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{4-4 i-1}{4+1}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow\frac{3}{5}+[/math]
 [math]\left(-\frac{4}{5}\right) \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{A}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{3}{5}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #329 (ID: 439276)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Dhaka Board | Tags: Dhaka Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]{Z}=2 \mathbf{x}+i .3 y ; x[/math] ও [math]y[/math] বাস্তব সংখ্যা হলে [math]|z|=1[/math] দ্বারা কি নির্দেশিত হয়?

Options:
ক. বৃত্ত
খ. উপবৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]|z|=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow|2 x+3 i y|=1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(2 x)^{2}+(3 y)^{2}}=1 \Rightarrow 4 x^{2}+9 y^{2}=1[/math]
যা একটি উপবৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Question #330 (ID: 439277)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Dinajpur Board | Tags: Dinajpur Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]p=x+i y[/math] হলে [math]|p+2|=3[/math] নির্দেশ করে—?

Options:
ক. বৃত্ত
খ. সরলরেখা
গ. প্যারাবোলা
ঘ. উপবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]|p+2|=3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow|x+i y+2|=3[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x+2)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=3[/math]
 [math]\therefore(x+2)^{2}+y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=3^{2}[/math]
 যা একটি বৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Explanation:

Question #331 (ID: 439278)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে, [math]\bar{z} \bar{z}=1[/math] সমীকরণের জ্যামিতিক রূপ কোনটি?

Options:
ক. অধিবৃত্ত
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. উপবৃত্ত
ঘ. পরাবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math](x+i y)(x-i y)=1 \Rightarrow x^{2}-(i y)^{2}=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=1 \text {, }[/math]
 যা একটি বৃত্তের সমীকরণ ।

Question #332 (ID: 439279)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x-2 i y[/math] হলে [math]z \bar{z}=7[/math] এর সঞ্চারপথ একটি—?

Options:
ক. পরাবৃত্ত
খ. উপবৃত্ত
গ. বৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{z}=\mathrm{x}-2 \mathrm{iy} \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \overline{\mathrm{z}}[/math]
 [math]=\mathrm{x}+2 \mathrm{i} \mathrm{y}[/math]
এখন , [math]{\bar{z}}=7[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow(x-2 i y)(x+2 i y)=7[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{2}+4 y^{2}=7[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{x^{2}}{7}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{y^{2}}{\frac{7}{4}}[/math]
 [math]=1[/math]
, যা একটি উপবৃত্তের সমীকরণ ।

Explanation:

 [math]\Rightarrow\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{6}} \sqrt{1+2 i+i^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{6}} \sqrt{(1+i)^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\pm \frac{1}{\sqrt{6}}(1+i)[/math]

 [math]=\frac{\sqrt{2}(\sqrt{i}+\sqrt{-i})}{\sqrt{-4 i^{2}}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\sqrt{2} \times \sqrt{2}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=1[/math]

Question:
এককের জটিল ঘনমূল x ও y হলে -

i. [math]x^{2}=y[/math]

ii. [math]x^{2}+y^{2}=i^{2}[/math]

iii. [math]x^{2} y^{2}=i^{4}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]x=\omega[/math]
 হলে, [math]y=\omega^{2}, x^{2}=\omega^{2}=y[/math]
 [math]\text{ii. } \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2}+\omega^{4}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2}+\omega=-1=\mathrm{i}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\text{iii. } x^{2} y^{2}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{2} \cdot \omega^{4}[/math]
 [math]=\omega^{6}=1=i^{4}[/math]

Question #343 (ID: 439290)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2019 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের তথ্যের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]z=-2 i[/math] একটি জটিল সংখ্যা ।

[math]\overline{\mathbf{z}}[/math] এর প্রতিরূপী বিন্দু কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math](-2,0)[/math]
খ. [math](0,-2)[/math]
গ. [math](2,0)[/math]
ঘ. [math](0,2)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{z}=-2 \mathrm{i} \quad[/math]
 [math]\therefore \overline{\mathrm{z}}=0+2 \mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]x=0, y=2[/math]
z এর প্রতিরূপী বিন্দু [math](0,2)[/math]


Explanation:

Question:

Options:
ক. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
খ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{3} ; \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
গ. [math]2 \mathrm{n} \pi-\frac{5 \pi}{3}, \mathrm{n} \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]
ঘ. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{5 \pi}{3}, n \in \mathbb{Z}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]1-\sqrt{3} i[/math]
 এর আর্গুমেন্ট [math]\theta[/math]
 হলে,[math]\theta=\tan ^{-1} \frac{-\sqrt{3}}{1}[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}(-\sqrt{3})[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}[/math]
 [math]\left\{\tan \left(2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}\right)\right\}[/math]
[জটিল সংখ্যাটির অবস্থান ৪র্থ চতুৰ্ভাগে][math]\Rightarrow 2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{3}[/math]
 যেখানে, [math]n[/math]
 পূর্ণ সংখ্যা

Question #346 (ID: 439293)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018 | Boards:  | Tags: All Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
কাল্পনিক সংখ্যা [math]i[/math] এবং [math]n \in \mathbb{N}[/math] এর জন্য [math]i^{4 n}-i+i^{4 n+1}-1[/math] এর মান কত?

Options:
ক. [math]-i[/math]
খ. [math]i[/math]
গ. [math]0[/math]
ঘ. [math]1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]i^{4 n}-i+i^{4 n+1}-1[/math]
 [math]=\left(i^{2}\right)^{2 n}-[/math]
 [math]i+i^{4 n}[/math]
 [math]\cdot i-1[/math]
 [math]=(-1)^{2 n}-i[/math]
 [math]+\left(i^{2}\right)^{2 n}[/math]
 [math]\cdot i-1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow1-i+(-1)^{2 n}[/math]
 [math]\cdot i-1[/math]
 [math]=1-i+i-1=0[/math]


Explanation:

Question #347 (ID: 439294)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2018 | Boards:  | Tags: All Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\alpha=\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} i}{2}[/math] এবং এর অনুবন্ধী [math]\bar{\alpha}[/math] হলে কোনটি সত্য?

Options:
ক. [math]\alpha \bar{\alpha}=\alpha^{2}[/math]
খ. [math]\alpha+\bar{\alpha}=2 \alpha[/math]
গ. [math]\alpha+\bar{\alpha}=-1[/math]
ঘ. [math]\bar{\alpha}+\alpha^{2}=-1[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\begin{array}{l}\alpha=\frac{-1+\sqrt{3} \mathrm{i}}{2}=-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \mathrm{i} \therefore \bar{\alpha}=-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \mathrm{i} \\\therefore \alpha+\bar{\alpha}=-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \mathrm{i}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2} \mathrm{i}=-1\end{array}[/math]

Explanation:

 এর আর্গুমেন্ট, [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{0}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\tan ^{-1}(\infty)=\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]

Question #349 (ID: 439296)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=\frac{1}{1+i}[/math] এর আর্গুমেন্ট কোনটি?

Options:
ক. [math]\frac{-3 \pi}{4}[/math]
খ. [math]\frac{-\pi}{4}[/math]
গ. [math]\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
ঘ. [math]\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{z}=\frac{1}{1+\mathrm{i}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-\mathrm{i}}{(1+\mathrm{i})(1-\mathrm{i})}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-\mathrm{i}}{1-\mathrm{i}^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-\mathrm{i}}{1+1}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1-\mathrm{i}}{2}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2} \mathrm{i}[/math]
এখন [math]\theta=\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{\frac{-1}{2}}{\frac{1}{2}}\right|=[/math]
 [math]\tan ^{-1} \tan \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 আর্গুমেণ্ট [math]=-\theta[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-\pi}{4}[/math]
 [যেহেতু চতুর্থ চতুর্ভাগে অবস্থিত।]

Explanation:

C. [math]-i[/math]
D. [math]i[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:

Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] ও [math]y[/math] বাস্তব সংখ্যা হলে [math]|z|^{2}=1[/math] দ্বারা কি নির্দেশিত হয় ?

Options:
ক. উপবৃত্ত
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. অধিবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]z=x+i y[/math]
 হলে [math]|z|=\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \quad|\mathrm{z}|^{2}=1 \Rightarrow \mathrm{x}^{2}+\mathrm{y}^{2}=1 ;[/math]
 যা বৃত্তের সমীকরণ।

Options:
A. [math]-2 \omega^{2}[/math]
B. [math]-2 \omega[/math]
C. [math]0[/math]
D. [math]3[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
যেহেতু [math]\omega^{3}=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{1}{\omega^{2015}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{2016}}+\frac{1}{\omega^{2017}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1}{\omega^{2}}+\frac{1}{1}+\frac{1}{\omega}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1+\omega^{2}+\omega}{\omega^{2}}=[/math]
 [math]\frac{0}{\omega^{2}}=0[/math]

Question #364 (ID: 439311)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2017 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board

Question:
এককের জটিল ঘনমূল দুইটি a ও b হলে—

[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } 1+a+b=0\\\text{ii. } a b=1 \text{.}\\\text{iii. } \mathrm{b}=\mathrm{a}^{2}\end{array}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. ii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
মনে করি, [math]a=\omega \text { ; } b=\omega^{2}[/math]
 হলে,[math]\begin{array}{l}\text{i. } 1+a+b=0 \Rightarrow 1+\omega+\omega^{2}=0\\\text{ii. } a b=\omega \cdot \omega^{2}=\omega^{3}=1\\\text{iii. } b=\omega^{2}=(\omega)^{2}=a^{2} \text{.}\end{array}[/math]

Question #367 (ID: 439315)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2023-2024 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
1-এর একটি জটিল ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math]\omega^{16}+\omega^{32}=?[/math]

Options:
ক. 1
খ. -2
গ. -1
ঘ. 2


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\omega^{16}+\omega^{32}=\left(\omega^{3}\right)^{5} \cdot \omega+\left(\omega^{3}\right)^{10} \cdot \omega^{2}=\omega+\omega^{2}=-1[/math]

Explanation:

Question:
[math]\frac{1+i}{1-i}[/math] এর পরম মান হলো-

Options:
ক. [math]0[/math]
খ. [math]1[/math]
গ. [math]\sqrt2[/math]
ঘ. [math]i[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1+i}{1-i}=\frac{(1+i)^2}{(1-i)(1+i)}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{1+2i-1}{2}=\mathrm{i}[/math]
 [math]\therefore\frac{1+i}{1-i}[/math]
 এর পরম মান [math]=mod (i)=\sqrt{0^{2}+1^{2}}=1[/math]

Question:
যদি [math]\mathrm{z}_1=1-\mathrm{i},\mathrm{z}_2=\sqrt{3}+i[/math] হয় তবে [math]\frac{z_{2}}{z_{1}}[/math] এর নতি-

Options:
A. [math]\frac{5 \pi}{12}[/math]
B. [math]\frac{\pi}{6}[/math]
C. [math]-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{5 \pi}{12}[/math]


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
[math]\frac{z_{2}}{z_{1}}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\sqrt{3}+i}{1-i}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \frac{z_{2}}{z_{1}}[/math]
 এর নতি [math]=[/math]
 ( [math]z_{2}[/math]
 এর নতি) [math]-[/math]
( [math]z_{1}[/math]
 এর নতি)[math]=\tan ^{-1}[/math]
 [math]\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\right)[/math]
 [math]-\tan ^{-1}(-1)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{6}-\left(-\frac{\pi}{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\frac{\pi}{6}+\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{5 \pi}{12}[/math]

Question:
[math]\text{ If }\frac{1}{a+i}=\frac{1}{a-i},\ then\ the\ value\ of\ a\ is[/math]-

B. [math]\frac{i}{2}[/math]
C. [math]-1[/math]
D. [math]-\frac{i}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: গ

Solution:
[math]\frac{1}{a+i}=\frac{1}{a-i}\\\Rightarrow\ a-i=ia+i^2\\\Rightarrow\ a-i=-1+ai,[/math]
বাস্তব অংশের সহগ সমীকৃত করে, [math]a=-1[/math]

 একটি বক্ররেখা যার প্রতিটি বিন্দুর [math](-5,0)[/math]
 ও [math](5,0)[/math]

। কাজেই এটি একটি উপবৃত্ত যার উপকেন্দ্রদ্বয় [math](\pm 5,0)[/math]
 ও বৃহদাক্ষের দৈর্ঘ্য [math]16[/math]
 একক।

Question:
[math]z=1-\frac{i}{1-\frac{1}{1+i}}[/math] জটিল সংখ্যাটির মডুলাস ও আর্গুমেন্ট-

Options:
ক. [math]1,0[/math]
খ. [math]1, \frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]1,\pi[/math]
ঘ. [math]1,\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math]z=1-\frac{i}{1-\frac{1}{1+i}}[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{i}{\frac{1+i-1}{1+i}}[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{i+i^2}{i}[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{i^2+i^3}{i^2}[/math]
 [math]=1-\frac{-1-i}{-1}[/math]
 [math]=1-1-i=-i[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 মডুলাস [math]=1[/math]
, আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=\frac{3 \pi}{2}[/math]


Explanation:

Question #375 (ID: 439323)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2015-2016, 2006-2007 | Boards:  | Tags: University of Dhaka (A Unit), University of Rajshahi (C Unit)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
এককের একটি কাল্পনিক ঘনমূল [math]\omega[/math] হলে [math](1-\omega)\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)\left(1-\omega^{4}\right)\left(1-\omega^{8}\right)[/math] এর মান-

Options:
A. [math]18[/math]
B. [math]6[/math]
C. [math]-9[/math]
D. [math]9[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
[math](1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{8}\right)[/math]
 [math]=(1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math](1-\omega)[/math]
 [math]\left(1-\omega^{2}\right)[/math]
 [math]=\left\{(1-\omega)\left(1-\omega^2\right)\right\}^2[/math]
 [math]=\left(1-\omega-\omega^2+\omega^3\right)^2[/math]
 [math]=(1+1+1)^2[/math]
 [math]=9[/math]


Explanation:

Question #376 (ID: 439259)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]- 1 + i[/math] এর পোলার আকার-

Options:
ক. [math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{4}+i \sin \frac{\pi}{4}\right)[/math]
খ. [math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{\pi}{4}-i \sin \frac{\pi}{4}\right)[/math]
গ. [math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 \pi}{4}-i \sin \frac{3 \pi}{4}\right)[/math]
ঘ. [math]\sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 \pi}{4}+i \sin \frac{3 \pi}{4}\right)[/math]


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
ধরি, [math]z=-1+i[/math]
এখন, [math]x=-1, y=1[/math]
 [math]\therefore|z|=r =\sqrt{(x)^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{(-1)^{2}+1^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{1+1}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{2}[/math]
এখানে, [math](–1, 1)[/math]
 বিন্দুটি ২য় চতুর্ভাগে অবস্থিত।[math]\therefore \arg z[/math]
 [math]=\theta[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{-1}\right|[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi-\tan ^{-1} 1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathbf{Z}[/math]
 এর পোলার সমীকরণ [math]=r^{}(\cos \theta+i \sin \theta)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 \pi}{4}+i \sin \frac{3 \pi}{4}\right) \text { [Ans.] }[/math]


Explanation:

Question #377 (ID: 439260)

Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Cumilla Board

Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে [math]|z-1|=5[/math] সমীকরণটি কী নির্দেশ করে ?

Options:
ক. সরলরেখা
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. উপবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]z=x+i y[/math]
এবং [math]|z-1|=5[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow |x+i y-1|=5 \\\Rightarrow |(x-1)+i y|=5 \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{(x-1)^{2}+y^{2}}=5\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore(x-1)^{2}+y^{2}=5^{2}[/math]
 [উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে][math]\therefore[/math]
 ইহা [math](1,0)[/math]
 কেন্দ্র এবং [math]5[/math]
 ব্যাসার্ধ বিশিষ্ট একটি বৃত্তের সমীকরণ নির্দেশ করে

Explanation:

Question #378 (ID: 439261)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে [math]|z|=5[/math] সমীকরণটি প্রকাশ করে-

Options:
ক. সরলরেখা
খ. বৃত্ত
গ. পরাবৃত্ত
ঘ. উপবৃত্ত


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
[math]\mathrm{z}=\mathrm{x}+\mathrm{iy}[/math]
এবং [math]|z|=5[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\Rightarrow |x+i y|=5\\\Rightarrow \sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}=5\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore x^{2}+y^{2}=5^{2}[/math]
 [ উভয়পক্ষকে বর্গ করে]

Question #379 (ID: 439262)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Jessore Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\mathbf{z}=\mathbf{i}-\mathbf{1}[/math] হলে-

[math]\begin{array}{l} \mathrm i . \overline{\mathrm{z}}=-\mathrm{i}-1 \\\mathrm i \mathrm i.| \mathrm{z}|=\sqrt{2}\end{array}[/math] [math]\text { iii. }[/math] z এর পোলার আকার [math]\cos \frac{\pi}{4}-i \sin \frac{\pi}{4}[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
এখানে, [math]\mathbf{z}=\mathbf{i}-1[/math]
ধরি, [math]z=x+i y=i-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \mathrm{x}=-1, \mathrm{y}=1 \\\overline{\mathrm{z}}=\overline{\mathrm{i}-1}=-\mathrm{i}-1[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক[math]|\mathrm{z}|=\mathrm{r}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{(-1)^{2}+1^{2}}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{1+1}[/math]
 [math]=\sqrt{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (ii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক।যেহেতু [math](–1, 1)[/math]
 বিন্দুটি দ্বিতীয় চতুর্ভাগে অবস্থিত।[math]\therefore \arg z[/math]
 [math]=\theta[/math]
 [math]=\pi-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{1}{-1}\right|[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi-\tan ^{-1} 1[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \pi-\frac{\pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]=\frac{3 \pi}{4}[/math]
 [math]\therefore Z[/math]
 এর পোলার আকার [math]i-1=r(\cos \theta+i \sin \theta)[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \sqrt{2}\left(\cos \frac{3 \pi}{4}+i \sin \frac{3 \pi}{4}\right)[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (iii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক নয়।

Question #380 (ID: 439263)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Chittagong Board | Tags: Chattogram Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]\sqrt[3]{1}[/math] এর মূলত্রয়ের-

i. যোগফল শূন্য

ii. দুইটি জটিল

iii. একটি মূল-অপর একটি মূলের বর্গের সমান

Options:
ক. i ও ii
খ. i ও iii
গ. ii ও iii
ঘ. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ঘ

Solution:
ধরি, [math]x=\sqrt[3]{1}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow x^{3}=1\\\Rightarrow x^{3}-1=0\\\Rightarrow x^{3}-1^{3}=0\\\Rightarrow (x-1)\left(x^{2}+x+1\right)=0[/math]
হয় [math]x-1=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x=1[/math]
অথবা, [math]x^{2}+x+1=0[/math]
 [math]\therefore x =\frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{1^{2}-4.1 .1}}{2.1}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{-1 \pm \sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \sqrt[3]{1}[/math]
 এর মূলত্রয় [math]1, \frac{-1+\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 এবং [math]\frac{-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
মূলত্রয়ের যোগফল [math]=1+\frac{-1+\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]+\frac{-1-\sqrt{3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{2-1+\sqrt{-3}-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{0}{2}=0=[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 মূল দুটি জটিল মূল।[math]\therefore \text { (ii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক[math]\frac{-1+\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 এবং [math]\frac{-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 [math]\frac{-1+\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 মূলটির বর্গ [math]=\left\{\frac{1}{2}(-1+\sqrt{-3})\right\}^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{4}(-1+\sqrt{-3})^{2}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{4}\left\{1+2 \cdot(-1) \cdot \sqrt{-3}+(\sqrt{-3})^{2}\right\}[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [math](1-2 \sqrt{-3}-3),[/math]
 [math]\Rightarrow \frac{1}{4}[/math]
 [math](-2-2 \sqrt{-3})[/math]
 [math]=\frac{-1-\sqrt{-3}}{2}[/math]
 যা অপর একটি মূলের সমান।[math]\therefore \text { (iii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক ।

Explanation:

Question #381 (ID: 439264)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards:  | Tags: Mymensingh Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
[math]z=x+i y[/math] হলে-

[math]\text { i. } |\mathrm{z}|=|\overline{\mathrm{z}}|[/math] [math]\text { ii. } \mathbf{z} . \bar{z}=|z|^{2}[/math] [math]\text { iii. } \arg (\bar{z})=\arg (z)[/math]

নিচের কোনটি সঠিক ?

Options:
A. i ও ii
B. i ও iii
C. ii ও iii
D. i, ii ও iii


Correct Answer: ক

Solution:
এখানে, [math]z=x+i y[/math]
 [math]|z|=\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore z=x+i y=x-i y \\\therefore|\vec{z}|=\sqrt{x^{2}+(-y)^{2}} \\\Rightarrow \sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (i) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক।[math]\text { z. } \bar{z}=(x+\text { iy })(x-i y)[/math]
 [math]\begin{aligned}\Rightarrow & x^{2}-i^{2} y^{2} \\\Rightarrow & x^{2}+y^{2} \\\therefore|z|^{2} & =\left(\sqrt{x^{2}+y^{2}}\right)^{2} \\& =x^{2}+y^{2}\end{aligned}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \text { (ii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক।এখানে, [math]z=x+i y[/math]
 হলে [math]\arg (z)=\tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}[/math]
 [math]\bar{z}=x-{i y}[/math]
 হলে [math]\arg (\bar{z})=-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{-y}{x}\right|[/math]
 [math][\because(1,-1)[/math]
 বিন্দুটি ৪র্থ চতুর্ভাগে অবস্থিত][math]\Rightarrow -\tan ^{-1} \frac{y}{x}[/math]
 [math]\therefore \arg (\overline{\mathrm{z}}) 
eq \arg (\mathrm{z}) \quad \therefore \text { (iii) }[/math]
 নং সঠিক নয় ।

Explanation:

Question #383 (ID: 439266)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Type: MCQ | Book: উচ্চতর গণিত ২য় পত্র | Years: 2022 | Boards: Rajshahi Board | Tags: Rajshahi Board
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Question:
নিচের উদ্দীপকের আলোকে প্রশ্নের উত্তর দাও:

[math]z=3 i[/math] [math]\overline{\mathbf{Z}}[/math] এর সাধারণ আর্গুমেন্ট কত?

Options:
ক. [math]2 n \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
খ. [math]2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
গ. [math]\mathrm{n} \pi+\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]
ঘ. [math]n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2}[/math]


Correct Answer: খ

Solution:
এখানে, [math]z=3 i[/math]
 [math]\begin{array}{l}\therefore \overline{\mathrm{z}}=\overline{3 \mathrm{i}}=-3 \mathrm{i}=0-3 \mathrm{i} \\\therefore \arg (\bar{z})=-\tan ^{-1}\left|\frac{-3}{0}\right| \\\Rightarrow -\tan ^{-1} \infty \\\Rightarrow -\frac{\pi}{2} \\\end{array}[/math]
 [math]\therefore[/math]
 সাধারণ আর্গুমেন্ট [math]=2 n \pi-\frac{\pi}{2} ; n \in \mathbb{N}[/math]


Explanation:
